   177	## Code Standards
   178	
   179	### Go (Backend)
   180	
   181	- Go 1.25+
   182	- Chi router for HTTP
   183	- pgx/v5 for PostgreSQL — raw SQL, no ORM
   184	- `internal/` package layout (not exported)
   185	- `cmd/server/` and `cmd/agent/` entry points
   186	- `cli/` — separate Go module for `ocm` CLI tool (own `go.mod`)
   187	- Static binary builds: `CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux`
   188	- Version injection via `-ldflags`
   189	
   190	### TypeScript (Frontend)
   191	
   192	- Strict mode enabled (`strict: true` in tsconfig)
   193	- No unused locals or parameters (`noUnusedLocals`, `noUnusedParameters`)
   194	- Path alias: `@/*` → `./src/*`
   195	- React JSX transform (`jsx: "react-jsx"`)
   196	- Target: ES2020
   197	- Vite for bundling, Vitest for testing
   198	- Tailwind CSS for styling
   199	- Radix UI for accessible components
   200	
   201	## Architecture Reference
   202	
   203	Split-plane architecture (from ClaraTeach):
   204	- **Control Plane** — stateless Go API on Cloud Run (port 8080)
   205	- **Worker Agents** — Go process on GCP Compute VMs (ports 9090/9091), manages Firecracker MicroVMs
   206	- **Each OpenClaw instance** = one Firecracker MicroVM (~125ms boot, KVM isolation)
   207	- **Networking** via Cloudflare Tunnel
   208	
   209	See `docs/architecture.md` for full details.
   210	
   211	### Configuration Pipeline
   212	
   213	`configassembly` package assembles gateway config from three layers: platform defaults → user config (pushed via API) → credential injection. The assembled config is served to VMs via the metadata server.
   214	
   215	## Artifact Delivery Chain
   216	
   217	GCS bucket → `manifest.json` → Cloud Run env var → provisioner → GCE instance metadata → agent reads metadata → downloads artifact.
   218	
   219	- **Rootfs and OpenClaw artifacts are decoupled from the agent.** New VMs pull the latest versions from GCS at start time. No agent restart is needed for rootfs or openclaw changes.
   220	- **Agent updates are manual.** After `make upload-agent`, hosts stay on their current version until an admin clicks the "Update" button on the AdminHosts page (one host at a time). The update restarts the agent via systemd; Firecracker VMs survive because the default runtime-owner is `systemd-unit` (each VM runs in its own per-VM unit, `ocm-vm-<id>.service` / `ocm-browser-vm-<id>.service`, independent of `ocm-agent.service`). `KillMode=process` on the agent unit further guards legacy direct-owner VMs from the cgroup SIGKILL. See `docs/plans/2026-04-11-vm-runtime-ownership-split.md`. Fresh hosts still pull the latest agent at first boot; only steady-state updates require the manual click. Only upload a new agent (`make upload-agent`) when agent code actually changed.
   221	- After `make build-upload-rootfs`, new VMs immediately get the new rootfs. Existing running VMs are unaffected.
   222	- To check agent logs: `gcloud compute ssh <VM> --zone=<ZONE> --command="sudo journalctl -u ocm-agent --since '30 min ago' | grep -i selfupdate"`
   223	
   224	## Init Script Environment Scoping (`scripts/init-openclaw.sh`)
   225	
   226	The init script has **three separate environment contexts**. Env vars needed by multiple contexts must be added to each:
   227	
   228	1. **Gateway env block** (`su -s /bin/bash openclaw -c "..."` starting the gateway) — has `OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_TOKEN`, `OCM_CONFIG_SOURCE`, etc.
   229	2. **PTY server env block** (`su -s /bin/bash openclaw -c "..."` starting `agent --pty-server`) — spawned commands (`openclaw tui`, `tail -f`) inherit this env, NOT shell profile
   230	3. **`/etc/profile.d/` scripts** — only sourced by login shells (`/bin/bash --login`), not direct command execution
   231	
   232	## Firecracker VM Environment
   233	
   234	- Minimal `/dev` — no udev, no `/dev/fd` symlink by default
   235	- Process substitution `>(tee ...)` needs `/dev/fd → /proc/self/fd` symlink created explicitly
   236	- Must `mount -t proc proc /proc` before `/dev/fd` symlink works
   237	- Init script is PID 1 — no systemd, no services, everything is manual
   238	- Gateway startup takes ~55s (Node.js module loading) — quick-start only skips sidecars
   239	
   240	## Common Fixes
   241	
   242	| Problem | Solution |
   243	|---------|----------|
   244	| Stale deployment | `make deploy-all` |
   245	| Agent not updating | `make upload-agent` (uploads to GCS), then click "Update" on the host row in AdminHosts. No polling — hosts do not auto-update. The agent restart is in-place; Firecracker VMs keep running under their per-VM systemd units. |
   246	| Rootfs stale on new VMs | `make build-upload-rootfs` — new VMs pull immediately from GCS, no agent restart needed |
   247	| VM not provisioning | Check GCP Console VM logs |
   248	| Frontend proxy errors | Ensure backend is running on port 8080 |
   249	| Need to rollback | `make list-agent` / `make rollback-agent VERSION=xxx` |
   250	| TUI can't connect to gateway | Check `OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_TOKEN` is in PTY server env block, not just profile.d |
   251

     1	# VM Resource Instrumentation — Design
     2	
     3	**Date:** 2026-04-29
     4	**Status:** Design — pending Codex review
     5	**Branch:** `instrumentation`
     6	**Goal:** Give end users live and historical CPU + memory charts for every VM they own (OpenClaw + Browser).
     7	
     8	## Why this matters to users
     9	
    10	When something feels slow inside their VM, the user has no way today to see whether it's CPU-bound, memory-pressured, or just network. They open `/machines/:id` and see static metadata — name, slug, tunnel URL, status — but no signal about how the VM is actually using its 2 vCPU / 2 GB. Rolling out a feature, debugging a hang, or sizing the next VM all benefit from a simple "what's it doing right now" view.
    11	
    12	This design adds a Resources tab on the machine detail page (and the equivalent for browser VMs) with two sub-views:
    13	
    14	1. **Live** — sliding 5-minute window, refreshing every second, with current-value big numbers above two line charts.
    15	2. **History** — pick a date range, see the full timeline, zoom and pan. Available even after a VM stops, for the retention window.
    16	
    17	Naming note: the existing `UsageTab.tsx` already covers token/billing usage. The new tab is **Resources**, not Usage, to avoid collision.
    18	
    19	## What gets measured
    20	
    21	For each VM (OpenClaw or Browser):
    22	
    23	- **CPU %** — derived from cgroup `cpu.stat` `usage_usec`, sampled at 1 Hz, computed as `(delta_usage_usec / delta_wall_usec) * 100`. May exceed 100 % for multi-core VMs (correct: a 2-vCPU VM at full load reads 200 %).
    24	- **Memory bytes** — cgroup `memory.current`, sampled at 1 Hz, instantaneous resident.
    25	
    26	Not in this phase: disk I/O, network bytes, page faults, GPU. Easy to add once the pipeline exists.
    27	
    28	## Architecture
    29	
    30	```
    31	                                                                 (every 1 s)
    32	[ host: ocm-agent ] ── 1 Hz sample ──▶ in-mem ring (30 s) ──┐
    33	                                                            │ POST every 10 s, batched
    34	                                                            ▼
    35	[ Cloud Run: backend ] ── insert ──▶ vm_metrics_1s (Postgres, daily partitions, 24 h)
    36	                          │
    37	                          │ minute-cron downsamples 1 s → 1 m
    38	                          ▼
    39	                     vm_metrics_1m (Postgres, daily partitions, 7 d)
    40	                          ▲
    41	[ user browser ] ─────────┴── GET /metrics?from=…&to=… (history)
    42	                ─────────────  GET /metrics/live (SSE, 1 s frames)
    43	```
    44	
    45	### Source of truth: cgroup v2 on the host
    46	
    47	Every VM already runs in its own systemd unit:
    48	
    49	- `ocm-vm-<machine-id>.service` for OpenClaw VMs
    50	- `ocm-browser-vm-<vm-id>.service` for Browser VMs
    51	
    52	These map to cgroup v2 paths under `/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/`. The two files we read are stable, kernel-owned, atomic-read:
    53	
    54	- `cpu.stat` — line-oriented, `usage_usec <N>` is the cumulative CPU time charged to the cgroup (microseconds, monotonic).
    55	- `memory.current` — single integer, current bytes.
    56	
    57	CPU% formula:
    58	
    59	```
    60	cpu_pct = (cur.usage_usec - prev.usage_usec) / (cur.wall_ns / 1000 - prev.wall_ns / 1000) * 100
    61	```
    62	
    63	Wall time is captured at sample time on the host clock (`time.Now()`), not from any kernel field. This handles the case where two consecutive reads happen 0.97 s and 1.04 s apart — the divisor uses the actual elapsed micros, not a hardcoded 1_000_000.
    64	
    65	### Discovery
    66	
    67	A `cgroup.Discover(root string) []VMRef` function scans `system.slice/` for entries matching `^ocm-(browser-)?vm-([0-9a-f-]+)\.service$`. It extracts the kind (`machine` vs `browser`) and the VM UUID. Cgroups that disappear between discovery and sample (because the VM stopped mid-tick) are dropped silently.
    68	
    69	Discovery runs every 10 s, not every 1 s — the cgroup set rarely changes.
    70	
    71	### Sampling loop (agent)
    72	
    73	A new package `backend/internal/vmmetrics/sampler` owns a single goroutine:
    74	
    75	```go
    76	type Sampler struct {
    77	    Root       string                  // /sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice
    78	    Tick       time.Duration           // 1 * time.Second
    79	    BatchEvery time.Duration           // 10 * time.Second
    80	    HostID     string
    81	    Pusher     Pusher                  // POST batch to backend
    82	    prev       map[string]Sample       // last sample per cgroup-name
    83	    buffer     []Point                 // ring, capacity 30s * #vms
    84	}
    85	```
    86	
    87	- 1 Hz tick: for each cgroup, read both files, compute deltas vs `prev`, append a `Point{vm_id, kind, ts, cpu_pct, mem_bytes}` to the buffer.
    88	- Every 10 s, drain the buffer into a batch, POST to backend. On non-2xx, requeue with exponential backoff (cap 60 s). On buffer full (>30 s of data unsent), drop oldest with a counter incremented (`vm_metrics_dropped_total`).
    89	- First sample for a new cgroup has no `prev`, so cpu_pct is omitted; memory is reported. Second tick produces the first cpu_pct.
    90	
    91	### Wire format (agent → backend)
    92	
    93	`POST /api/v1/agent/vm-metrics`
    94	
    95	```json
    96	{
    97	  "host_id": "h-east-3",
    98	  "points": [
    99	    {
   100	      "vm_id": "9f3c…",
   101	      "kind": "machine",
   102	      "ts": "2026-04-29T18:33:01.000Z",
   103	      "cpu_pct": 12.4,
   104	      "mem_bytes": 524288000
   105	    },
   106	    …up to ~200 points per host…
   107	  ]
   108	}
   109	```
   110	
   111	Auth: same Bearer token the agent already sends for heartbeat (`AgentToken`), validated against the host row's stored token. Backend rejects points whose `vm_id` is not currently placed on this host (defense against a compromised agent injecting metrics for VMs it doesn't own).
   112	
   113	`kind` is `"machine"` or `"browser"`. The two VM tables (`machines` / `browser_vms`) live in different namespaces, so the discriminator is needed for FK validation and routing.
   114	
   115	### Storage schema
   116	
   117	Two tables, each partitioned by day on `ts`. New migration: `084_vm_metrics.sql`.
   118	
   119	```sql
   120	CREATE TABLE vm_metrics_1s (
   121	    vm_id      UUID         NOT NULL,
   122	    kind       TEXT         NOT NULL,   -- 'machine' | 'browser'
   123	    ts         TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL,
   124	    cpu_pct    REAL         NOT NULL,
   125	    mem_bytes  BIGINT       NOT NULL,
   126	    PRIMARY KEY (vm_id, ts)
   127	) PARTITION BY RANGE (ts);
   128	
   129	CREATE INDEX vm_metrics_1s_kind_ts ON vm_metrics_1s (kind, ts);
   130	
   131	CREATE TABLE vm_metrics_1m (
   132	    vm_id      UUID         NOT NULL,
   133	    kind       TEXT         NOT NULL,
   134	    ts         TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL,    -- bucket start, minute-aligned
   135	    cpu_pct    REAL         NOT NULL,    -- avg over the minute
   136	    mem_bytes  BIGINT       NOT NULL,    -- avg over the minute
   137	    PRIMARY KEY (vm_id, ts)
   138	) PARTITION BY RANGE (ts);
   139	```
   140	
   141	No FK to `machines.id` or `browser_vms.id` — the metrics outlive the VMs (history view should still work after a VM is deleted, until retention drops it).
   142	
   143	Daily partitions named `vm_metrics_1s_y2026m04d29`. A maintenance cron creates the next 3 days of partitions ahead of time and drops partitions older than retention.
   144	
   145	### Cron jobs (backend)
   146	
   147	A new package `backend/internal/vmmetrics/maint` exposes three jobs that the existing fleet scheduler invokes:
   148	
   149	| Job | Frequency | What it does |
   150	|-----|-----------|--------------|
   151	| `EnsurePartitions` | every 1 h | Create partitions for `today + 0..3` days for both tables. Idempotent. |
   152	| `Downsample1s1m` | every 1 min | Insert 1-min buckets for the 1 s rows from `now() - 65s` to `now() - 5s` that aren't already in `vm_metrics_1m`. `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`. |
   153	| `DropOldPartitions` | every 1 h | `DROP TABLE` 1 s partitions older than 24 h, 1 m partitions older than 7 d. |
   154	
   155	The 65 s / 5 s window for downsample tolerates clock skew and late-arriving batches. Once a minute-bucket exists in `vm_metrics_1m`, it's never updated — late points within the bucket are discarded. Acceptable: the drop is bounded by batch size (10 s) so the worst-case error in a 60 s avg is one data point.
   156	
   157	### User-facing endpoints
   158	
   159	All three endpoints check that the caller's account owns the VM. 403 on mismatch.
   160	
   161	#### History
   162	
   163	```
   164	GET /api/v1/machines/:id/metrics?from=<ISO>&to=<ISO>
   165	GET /api/v1/browser-vms/:id/metrics?from=<ISO>&to=<ISO>
   166	```
   167	
   168	Server picks resolution by range:
   169	
   170	- `to - from <= 1h` → query `vm_metrics_1s`
   171	- `to - from > 1h` → query `vm_metrics_1m`
   172	
   173	If a range straddles the 1 s retention boundary (e.g., `from = -25h, to = now`), the server splits the query: 1 m for the older portion, 1 s for the last 24 h. The response carries `resolution: "1s" | "1m" | "mixed"`.
   174	
   175	Hard cap: `to - from <= 7d` (the longer-retention window). Larger ranges return 400.
   176	
   177	Response:
   178	
   179	```json
   180	{
   181	  "vm_id": "…",
   182	  "resolution": "1s",
   183	  "from": "…",
   184	  "to": "…",
   185	  "points": [{"ts": "…", "cpu_pct": 12.4, "mem_bytes": 524288000}, …]
   186	}
   187	```
   188	
   189	#### Live (SSE)
   190	
   191	```
   192	GET /api/v1/machines/:id/metrics/live
   193	GET /api/v1/browser-vms/:id/metrics/live
   194	```
   195	
   196	Server-Sent Events stream. On open, the server emits the last 5 minutes of 1 s data immediately as one event, then continues to emit one event per second as new data lands.
   197	
   198	Implementation: backend tails `vm_metrics_1s` with a 1 s polling query (`WHERE vm_id = $1 AND ts > $2 ORDER BY ts`). Cheap because the index covers it and the per-second batch from agents lands within ~10 s.
   199	
   200	We deliberately do **not** ask the agent to push directly to the user — too many edge cases (auth, NAT, agent restarts). Pushing through the backend keeps a single auth surface and lets the user disconnect/reconnect cheaply.
   201	
   202	### Frontend
   203	
   204	New tab `ResourcesTab.tsx` registered in `MachineView.tsx` and `BrowserVMLivePage.tsx`. Two sub-tabs (regular Radix `<Tabs>`):
   205	
   206	- **Live** (default)
   207	  - Two big-number readouts at the top: "CPU 14.2 %", "Memory 512 MB / 2 GB" (memory shows total from the VM's `memory_mb` field).
   208	  - Two stacked Recharts `<LineChart>` components, 5-minute window.
   209	  - SSE `EventSource` connection. Buffers points client-side, drops anything older than 5 min on each tick.
   210	- **History**
   211	  - Date+time range picker (default: last 24 h).
   212	  - Single `<LineChart>` per metric, full range.
   213	  - Resolution badge: "1-second" or "1-minute".
   214	  - Wheel-zoom + drag-pan (Recharts `Brush` component is a simpler first cut).
   215	
   216	Add `recharts` to `frontend/package.json`. No other frontend deps.
   217	
   218	### Naming and FK awareness
   219	
   220	The `kind` column avoids ambiguity at query time. The user-facing endpoints take a `vm_id` and *know* whether they're hitting `/machines/:id` or `/browser-vms/:id`, so they always filter `kind = 'machine'` or `kind = 'browser'` along with `vm_id` for safety, even though the UUID alone is unique across both tables in practice.
   221	
   222	## Test plan
   223	
   224	### Backend unit (Go)
   225	
   226	- `cgroup.ParseCPUStat` — fixture text parses correctly, missing `usage_usec` field returns error.
   227	- `cgroup.ParseMemoryCurrent` — single-integer file parses; whitespace tolerated.
   228	- `cgroup.CPUPercent(prev, curr)` — known deltas → known %, including >100 % multi-core case and the divide-by-zero case (same wall time → returns 0).
   229	- `cgroup.Discover(root)` — fixture dir with mixed `ocm-vm-*`, `ocm-browser-vm-*`, `unrelated.service` entries → only the VM units are returned, kind classified correctly.
   230	- `sampler.Tick` — fed two synthetic samples, produces one point with the expected fields.
   231	- `store.InsertVMMetrics` (integration, real Postgres via testcontainers) — round-trips a 200-point batch.
   232	- `store.QueryVMMetrics` — given seeded data, returns the right slice for a range; resolution selection picks `_1s` for ≤1h, `_1m` for >1h.
   233	- `maint.Downsample1s1m` — seed 60 1 s points across one minute, run, expect one 1 m row with the correct avg. Run again — no-op (idempotent).
   234	- `maint.DropOldPartitions` — seed daily partitions for last 30 days, run, expect 1 s partitions older than 1 day and 1 m partitions older than 7 days dropped.
   235	- `maint.EnsurePartitions` — fresh DB, run, expect today + 3 future partitions to exist on both tables.
   236	
   237	### Backend HTTP (Go)
   238	
   239	- `POST /api/v1/agent/vm-metrics` — bearer-auth path: missing token 401, wrong token 401, wrong host_id 403, valid → 204 + rows in DB.
   240	- `POST` — points referencing a `vm_id` not placed on this host are rejected (403 or filtered with a count in the response — TBD).
   241	- `GET /machines/:id/metrics` — owner gets points; non-owner gets 403; deleted-VM range still returns historical points.
   242	- `GET /machines/:id/metrics?from=…&to=…` — straddle range returns `resolution: "mixed"` and merged points.
   243	- `GET /machines/:id/metrics/live` — SSE: client receives initial backfill event then per-second updates; closes cleanly on context cancel.
   244	
   245	### Frontend (Vitest + Testing Library)
   246	
   247	- `ResourcesTab` renders the Live sub-tab by default with mocked SSE source emitting 5 frames; both charts render data.
   248	- `ResourcesTab` History sub-tab — given mocked `getMachineMetrics` returning 60 points, line chart renders with correct domain.
   249	- Range picker switches resolution badge from "1-second" to "1-minute" when the range exceeds 1 h.
   250	- SSE reconnect: when the EventSource fires `error`, the component shows a "reconnecting…" indicator and reattaches.
   251	
   252	### End-to-end smoke (manual, dev host)
   253	
   254	1. Provision a fresh VM, open Resources tab → live values appear within ~10 s.
   255	2. `stress-ng --cpu 2 --timeout 30s` inside the VM → CPU chart spikes to ~200 %.
   256	3. Allocate 500 MB inside the VM (`head -c 500M /dev/zero | tail`) → Memory chart steps up.
   257	4. Stop the VM → live view shows "VM stopped"; history view still loads the last 30 minutes.
   258	5. Wait 1 day → history at "last 7 days" shows 1-min resolution data for the day-old portion and the data from before the partition drop is gone (no errors).
   259	
   260	## Build sequence
   261	
   262	Each step is a logical commit. Keep them small.
   263	
   264	1. **Migration** — `084_vm_metrics.sql` + initial daily partitions. Tests for `Store.InsertVMMetrics`/`QueryVMMetrics`.
   265	2. **cgroup library** — `vmmetrics/cgroup` with parse + discover + percent. Pure, fixture-driven tests.
   266	3. **Sampler** — `vmmetrics/sampler` with the goroutine, ring, batch. Tests use a fake `Pusher`.
   267	4. **Agent integration** — wire sampler into `cmd/agent/main.go`, gated on `cfg.BackendURL && cfg.HostID && cfg.AgentToken` like heartbeat.
   268	5. **Backend ingest endpoint** — `POST /api/v1/agent/vm-metrics` with auth + placement check.
   269	6. **Maintenance crons** — `EnsurePartitions`, `Downsample1s1m`, `DropOldPartitions` wired into the fleet scheduler.
   270	7. **User history endpoint** — `GET /machines/:id/metrics` + `GET /browser-vms/:id/metrics`.
   271	8. **User SSE endpoint** — `GET /machines/:id/metrics/live` + browser equivalent.
   272	9. **Frontend** — add Recharts, build `ResourcesTab`, register in machine + browser-vm pages.
   273	10. **End-to-end smoke** on east host; commit screenshots in PR.
   274	
   275	## Risks and open questions
   276	
   277	- **Cardinality at 1 Hz**: 100 concurrent VMs → 8.6 M rows/day in `vm_metrics_1s`, ~430 MB before pg overhead. Daily partitions cap query scan to one day. If write rate becomes a problem we can reduce the tick to 2 s or push aggregates from agent.
   278	- **SSE through Cloud Run**: Cloud Run terminates idle connections after 60 min. SSE handler emits a heartbeat comment every 30 s to keep the proxy from buffering, and the client transparently reconnects on EOF.
   279	- **VM moves between hosts**: each batch is host-scoped. If a VM moves, the new host's agent picks up its cgroup and continues sampling — no special handling. The 1 s history just has a few-second gap during the move.
   280	- **Legacy direct-owner VMs (pre runtime-ownership-split)**: their cgroup isn't `system.slice/ocm-vm-*.service`. Sampler won't see them. Acceptable — those are being migrated out anyway, and this feature is explicitly forward-looking.
   281	- **Privacy**: CPU/memory of one user's VM is not visible to others — strictly account-scoped at the API layer. The agent → backend ingest doesn't carry user identity at all; ownership is resolved server-side via host placement.
   282	- **Storage growth past Phase 1**: at 1 m × 7 d × 50 VMs = 0.5 M rows total in `vm_metrics_1m`. Fine. If we later add disk and network we'd add columns or tables; design accommodates either.
   283	
   284	## Out of scope (Phase 2+)
   285	
   286	- Disk I/O, network counters, page faults — same pipeline, more cgroup fields.
   287	- Alerting ("ping me if my VM is at 95 % CPU for 5 min").
   288	- Aggregated views (account-wide rollup, host-wide rollup).
   289	- Export to Prometheus / OpenTelemetry — straightforward once the schema is stable.

    85	// maxVCPUOversubRatio is the upper bound for the vCPU oversubscription ratio,
    86	// matching the capacity_policies_cpu_ratio constraint (BETWEEN 1.0 AND 3.0).
    87	const maxVCPUOversubRatio = 3
    88	
    89	// vcpuOversubscriptionRatio reads VCPU_OVERSUBSCRIPTION_RATIO from the
    90	// environment, validates it, and returns a safe value clamped to [1, 3].
    91	func vcpuOversubscriptionRatio() int {
    92		raw := os.Getenv("VCPU_OVERSUBSCRIPTION_RATIO")
    93		if raw == "" {
    94			return 2 // default: 2x oversubscription
    95		}
    96		v, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
    97		if err != nil {
    98			slog.Warn("vcpu_oversub_ratio.invalid", "value", raw, "error", err, "using_default", 2)
    99			return 2
   100		}
   101		if v < 1 {
   102			slog.Warn("vcpu_oversub_ratio.below_minimum", "value", v, "clamped_to", 1)
   103			return 1
   104		}
   105		if v > maxVCPUOversubRatio {
   106			slog.Warn("vcpu_oversub_ratio.exceeds_max", "value", v, "max", maxVCPUOversubRatio, "clamped_to", maxVCPUOversubRatio)
   107			return maxVCPUOversubRatio
   108		}
   109		return v
   110	}

   112	// NewWorkerServer creates a Server with the dependencies needed for workflow
   113	// execution in worker-only mode (no HTTP router, no auth, no CORS).
   114	// Migration workflows need agentClient, placement, machines, and kvStore.
   115	func NewWorkerServer(s store.Store, placementSvc *fleet.PlacementService, agentCli *agentclient.Client, kv *kvstore.KVStore, tunnelMgr *tunnel.Manager, machineRuntime *machines.RuntimeService, backupMasterKey string) *Server {
   116		srv := &Server{
   117			store:           s,
   118			placement:       placementSvc,
   119			agentClient:     agentCli,
   120			kvStore:         kv,
   121			tunnelMgr:       tunnelMgr,
   122			machines:        machineRuntime,
   123			backupMasterKey: backupMasterKey,
   124		}
   125		srv.opikAPIURL = os.Getenv("OPIK_API_URL")
   126		if srv.opikAPIURL == "" {
   127			srv.opikAPIURL = os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL")
   128			if srv.opikAPIURL != "" {
   129				srv.opikAPIURL += "/api/opik"
   130			}
   131		}
   132		// Workers execute migration workflows that start/restart VMs, so they need
   133		// the same RuntimeService callbacks as the full API server.
   134		if machineRuntime != nil {
   135			machineRuntime.OnRunning = func(machineID string, isRestart bool) {
   136				ctx := context.Background()
   137				machine, err := srv.store.GetMachine(ctx, machineID)
   138				if err != nil {
   139					slog.Error("on_running.get_machine_failed", "machine_id", machineID, "error", err)
   140					return
   141				}
   142				if isRestart {
   143					slog.Info("on_running.restart_detected", "machine_id", machineID)
   144					if err := srv.waitForGatewayHealth(ctx, machine, 90*time.Second); err != nil {
   145						slog.Warn("on_running.gateway_not_ready", "machine_id", machineID, "error", err)
   146					}
   147					// Push config after restart to reflect any DB changes (e.g. auto-unpaired
   148					// browser VM) that happened before boot. The data volume has stale config
   149					// from the previous run; this ensures the running VM gets current state.
   150					srv.pushMachineConfigAsync(machineID)
   151				}
   152			}
   153		}
   154		if tunnelMgr != nil {
   155			srv.tunnelCreator = tunnelMgr
   156		}
   157		return srv
   158	}

   225			srv.tunnelCreator = tunnelMgr
   226		}
   227	
   228		// Wire function callbacks into the RuntimeService that depend on Server methods
   229		if machineRuntime != nil {
   230			machineRuntime.OnRunning = func(machineID string, isRestart bool) {
   231				ctx := context.Background()
   232				machine, err := srv.store.GetMachine(ctx, machineID)

    22	func (s *Server) reconcileHostBrowserPairings(ctx context.Context, host *store.Host) {
    23		if s.agentClient == nil || host == nil {
    24			return
    25		}
    26	
    27		machines, err := s.store.ListMachinesByHost(ctx, host.ID)
    28		if err != nil {
    29			slog.Warn("heartbeat.browser_pairings.list_machines_failed", "host_id", host.ID, "error", err)
    30			return
    31		}
    32	
    33		for _, machine := range machines {
    34			if machine.Status != "running" || machine.BrowserVMID == nil || machine.VMIP == nil {
    35				continue
    36			}
    37	
    38			bvm, err := s.store.GetBrowserVM(ctx, *machine.BrowserVMID)
    39			if err != nil {
    40				slog.Warn("heartbeat.browser_pairings.get_browser_vm_failed",
    41					"host_id", host.ID,
    42					"machine_id", machine.ID,
    43					"browser_vm_id", *machine.BrowserVMID,
    44					"error", err)
    45				continue
    46			}
    47			if bvm == nil || bvm.Status != "running" || bvm.VMIP == nil || bvm.HostID == nil || *bvm.HostID != host.ID {
    48				continue
    49			}
    50	
    51			if err := s.agentClient.PairBrowserVM(ctx, host, bvm.ID, *machine.VMIP); err != nil {
    52				slog.Warn("heartbeat.browser_pairings.replay_pair_failed",
    53					"host_id", host.ID,
    54					"machine_id", machine.ID,
    55					"browser_vm_id", bvm.ID,
    56					"machine_vm_ip", *machine.VMIP,
    57					"browser_vm_ip", *bvm.VMIP,
    58					"error", err)
    59				continue
    60			}
    61	
    62			cdpTarget := fmt.Sprintf("%s:9222", *bvm.VMIP)
    63			if err := s.agentClient.SetCDPTarget(ctx, host, machine.ID, cdpTarget); err != nil {
    64				slog.Warn("heartbeat.browser_pairings.replay_cdp_target_failed",
    65					"host_id", host.ID,
    66					"machine_id", machine.ID,
    67					"browser_vm_id", bvm.ID,
    68					"target", cdpTarget,
    69					"error", err)
    70				continue
    71			}
    72	
    73			slog.Info("heartbeat.browser_pairings.replayed",
    74				"host_id", host.ID,
    75				"machine_id", machine.ID,
    76				"browser_vm_id", bvm.ID,
    77				"machine_vm_ip", *machine.VMIP,
    78				"target", cdpTarget)
    79		}
    80	}
    81	
    82	func (s *Server) ServiceTokenMiddleware(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
    83		return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    84			if s.cfServiceTokenID == "" {
    85				writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, "service token not configured")
    86				return
    87			}
    88	
    89			clientID := r.Header.Get("CF-Access-Client-Id")
    90			clientSecret := r.Header.Get("CF-Access-Client-Secret")
    91	
    92			idMatch := subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(clientID), []byte(s.cfServiceTokenID)) == 1
    93			secMatch := subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(clientSecret), []byte(s.cfServiceTokenSec)) == 1
    94	
    95			if !idMatch || !secMatch {
    96				writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, "invalid service token")
    97				return
    98			}
    99			next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
   100		})
   101	}
   102	
   103	func (s *Server) handleInternalResolve(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   104		var req struct {
   105			AccountSlug string `json:"account_slug"`
   106			MachineSlug string `json:"machine_slug"`
   107		}
   108		if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
   109			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid request body")
   110			return
   111		}
   112		if req.AccountSlug == "" || req.MachineSlug == "" {
   113			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "account_slug and machine_slug are required")
   114			return
   115		}
   116	
   117		route, err := s.store.ResolveRoute(r.Context(), req.AccountSlug, req.MachineSlug)
   118		if err != nil {
   119			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "route not found")
   120			return
   121		}
   122	
   123		// Self-heal KV cache via RouteService (best-effort, already returning DB result)
   124		if s.routing != nil {
   125			if route.HostHostname != "" {
   126				_ = s.routing.SyncRouteToKV(r.Context(), routing.SyncKVRequest{
   127					AccountSlug:  req.AccountSlug,
   128					MachineSlug:  req.MachineSlug,
   129					MachineID:    route.MachineID,
   130					HostHostname: route.HostHostname,
   131					ProxyToken:   route.ProxyToken,
   132				})
   133			}
   134			_ = s.routing.SyncAccountToKV(r.Context(), req.AccountSlug, route.AccountID, route.UserIDs)
   135		}
   136	
   137		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, route)
   138	}
   139	
   140	// authenticateAgent validates the agent bearer token. It checks per-host token first
   141	// (if hostID is provided), then falls back to fleet-wide token.
   142	func (s *Server) authenticateAgent(ctx context.Context, r *http.Request, hostID int) (bool, error) {
   143		authHeader := r.Header.Get("Authorization")
   144		if !strings.HasPrefix(authHeader, "Bearer ") {
   145			return false, fmt.Errorf("missing bearer token")
   146		}
   147		token := strings.TrimPrefix(authHeader, "Bearer ")
   148	
   149		// Check per-host token first
   150		if hostID != 0 {
   151			host, err := s.store.GetHost(ctx, hostID)
   152			if err == nil && host.AgentToken != nil && *host.AgentToken != "" {
   153				if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(token), []byte(*host.AgentToken)) == 1 {
   154					return true, nil
   155				}
   156				return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid agent token for host")
   157			}
   158		}
   159	
   160		// Fall back to fleet-wide token
   161		if s.agentToken == "" {
   162			return false, fmt.Errorf("agent token not configured")
   163		}
   164		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(token), []byte(s.agentToken)) != 1 {
   165			return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid agent token")
   166		}
   167		return true, nil
   168	}
   169	
   170	// handleAgentHeartbeat receives periodic heartbeats from host agents.
   171	// Auth: Bearer token must match FC_AGENT_TOKEN or per-host agent_token.
   172	func (s *Server) handleAgentHeartbeat(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   173		var req struct {
   174			HostID               json.Number `json:"host_id"`
   175			ExternalIP           string      `json:"external_ip"`
   176			AgentVersion         string      `json:"agent_version"`
   177			VMCount              int         `json:"vm_count"`
   178			RootfsVersion        string      `json:"rootfs_version"`
   179			BrowserRootfsVersion string      `json:"browser_rootfs_version"`
   180			AgentEndpoint        string      `json:"agent_endpoint,omitempty"`
   181			OpenclawVersion      string      `json:"openclaw_version,omitempty"`
   182			VMVersions           map[string]struct {
   183				Rootfs   string `json:"rootfs,omitempty"`
   184				OpenClaw string `json:"openclaw,omitempty"`
   185			} `json:"vm_versions,omitempty"`
   186		}
   187		if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
   188			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid request body")
   189			return
   190		}
   191		hostID, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.Trim(req.HostID.String(), `"`))
   192		if err != nil || hostID == 0 || req.ExternalIP == "" {
   193			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "host_id and external_ip are required")
   194			return
   195		}
   196	
   197		ctx := r.Context()
   198	
   199		// Authenticate: check per-host token first, then fall back to fleet-wide token
   200		if ok, authErr := s.authenticateAgent(ctx, r, hostID); !ok {
   201			if authErr != nil && authErr.Error() == "missing bearer token" {
   202				writeError(w, http.StatusUnauthorized, authErr.Error())
   203			} else {
   204				writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, authErr.Error())
   205			}
   206			return
   207		}
   208	
   209		ipChanged, err := s.store.UpdateHostHeartbeat(ctx, hostID, req.ExternalIP, req.AgentVersion, req.RootfsVersion, req.BrowserRootfsVersion, req.OpenclawVersion)
   210		if err != nil {
   211			slog.Error("heartbeat.update_failed", "host_id", hostID, "error", err)
   212			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "heartbeat update failed")
   213			return
   214		}
   215	
   216		// Persist artifact state for observability.
   217		if req.RootfsVersion != "" {
   218			_ = s.store.UpsertHostArtifactState(ctx, &store.HostArtifactState{
   219				HostID:        hostID,
   220				Kind:          "rootfs",
   221				StagedVersion: strPtr(req.RootfsVersion),
   222			})
   223		}
   224		if req.OpenclawVersion != "" {
   225			_ = s.store.UpsertHostArtifactState(ctx, &store.HostArtifactState{
   226				HostID:        hostID,
   227				Kind:          "openclaw",
   228				StagedVersion: strPtr(req.OpenclawVersion),
   229			})
   230		}
   231		if updater, ok := s.store.(machineActualVersionUpdater); ok {
   232			for machineID, versions := range req.VMVersions {
   233				machineID = strings.TrimSpace(machineID)
   234				if machineID == "" {
   235					continue
   236				}
   237				if err := updater.UpdateMachineActualVersions(ctx, hostID, machineID, strings.TrimSpace(versions.Rootfs), strings.TrimSpace(versions.OpenClaw)); err != nil {
   238					slog.Warn("heartbeat.machine_versions_update_failed", "host_id", hostID, "machine_id", machineID, "error", err)
   239				}
   240			}
   241		}
   242	
   243		// Update agent_endpoint if reported
   244		if req.AgentEndpoint != "" {
   245			if err := s.store.UpdateHostAgentEndpoint(ctx, hostID, req.AgentEndpoint, "public_http"); err != nil {
   246				slog.Warn("heartbeat.update_endpoint_failed", "host_id", hostID, "error", err)
   247			}
   248		}
   249	
   250		if ipChanged {
   251			slog.Warn("heartbeat.ip_changed", "host_id", hostID, "new_ip", req.ExternalIP)
   252			s.activity.Log(ctx, events.LogParams{
   253				Category:  "host",
   254				Action:    "host.ip_changed",
   255				Status:    "success",
   256				ActorType: "agent",
   257				ActorID:   &hostID,
   258				HostID:    &hostID,
   259				Summary:   fmt.Sprintf("Host IP changed to %s", req.ExternalIP),
   260				Detail:    map[string]any{"new_ip": req.ExternalIP},

    18	
    19	// authenticateAgentToken validates the bearer token against per-host or fleet-wide tokens.
    20	// Returns the host ID on success (0 for fleet-wide), or writes an error and returns false.
    21	func (s *Server) authenticateAgentToken(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) (int, bool) {
    22		authHeader := r.Header.Get("Authorization")
    23		if !strings.HasPrefix(authHeader, "Bearer ") {
    24			writeError(w, http.StatusUnauthorized, "missing bearer token")
    25			return 0, false
    26		}
    27		token := strings.TrimPrefix(authHeader, "Bearer ")
    28	
    29		// Try per-host token lookup
    30		host, err := s.store.GetHostByAgentToken(r.Context(), token)
    31		if err == nil && host != nil {
    32			return host.ID, true
    33		}
    34	
    35		// Fall back to fleet-wide token
    36		if s.agentToken != "" && subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(token), []byte(s.agentToken)) == 1 {
    37			return 0, true
    38		}
    39	
    40		writeError(w, http.StatusUnauthorized, "invalid agent token")
    41		return 0, false
    42	}
    43	
    44	// validateMachinePlacement verifies that a machine is currently placed on the given host.
    45	// For fleet-wide tokens (hostID=0), the check is skipped.
    46	func (s *Server) validateMachinePlacement(ctx context.Context, machineID string, hostID int) error {
    47		if hostID == 0 {
    48			return nil
    49		}
    50		machine, err := s.store.GetMachine(ctx, machineID)
    51		if err != nil {
    52			return fmt.Errorf("machine not found")
    53		}
    54		if machine.HostID == nil || *machine.HostID != hostID {
    55			return fmt.Errorf("machine is not placed on this host")
    56		}
    57		return nil
    58	}
    59	
    60	func (s *Server) handleAgentAuthListAgents(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    61		hostID, ok := s.authenticateAgentToken(w, r)
    62		if !ok {
    63			return
    64		}
    65	
    66		machineID := chi.URLParam(r, "machineID")
    67		if err := s.validateMachinePlacement(r.Context(), machineID, hostID); err != nil {
    68			writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, err.Error())
    69			return
    70		}
    71	
    72		agents, err := s.store.ListMachineAgents(r.Context(), machineID)
    73		if err != nil {
    74			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, err.Error())
    75			return
    76		}
    77		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, agents)
    78	}

    80	func (s *Server) handleAgentAuthCreateAgent(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    81		hostID, ok := s.authenticateAgentToken(w, r)
    82		if !ok {
    83			return
    84		}
    85	
    86		machineID := chi.URLParam(r, "machineID")
    87		if err := s.validateMachinePlacement(r.Context(), machineID, hostID); err != nil {
    88			writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, err.Error())
    89			return
    90		}
    91	
    92		var req struct {
    93			AgentID        string  `json:"agent_id"`
    94			Name           string  `json:"name"`
    95			Model          *string `json:"model,omitempty"`
    96			IdentityEmoji  *string `json:"identity_emoji,omitempty"`
    97			IdentityAvatar *string `json:"identity_avatar,omitempty"`
    98			Soul           *string `json:"soul,omitempty"`
    99			IsDefault      bool    `json:"is_default"`
   100			SortOrder      int     `json:"sort_order"`
   101		}
   102		if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
   103			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid request body")
   104			return
   105		}
   106		if req.AgentID == "" || req.Name == "" {
   107			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "agent_id and name are required")
   108			return
   109		}
   110		if !validAgentID.MatchString(req.AgentID) {
   111			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "agent_id must be lowercase alphanumeric with hyphens, starting with a letter")
   112			return
   113		}
   114		machine, err := s.store.GetMachine(r.Context(), machineID)
   115		if err != nil {
   116			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "machine not found")
   117			return
   118		}
   119		if err := s.validateAgentModel(r.Context(), machineID, machine.AccountID, req.Model); err != nil {
   120			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, err.Error())
   121			return
   122		}
   123	
   124		agent := &store.MachineAgent{
   125			MachineID:      machineID,
   126			AgentID:        req.AgentID,
   127			Name:           req.Name,
   128			Model:          req.Model,
   129			IdentityEmoji:  req.IdentityEmoji,
   130			IdentityAvatar: req.IdentityAvatar,
   131			Soul:           req.Soul,
   132			IsDefault:      req.IsDefault,
   133			SortOrder:      req.SortOrder,
   134		}
   135	
   136		if err := s.store.CreateMachineAgent(r.Context(), agent); err != nil {
   137			if isUniqueViolation(err) {
   138				writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, "agent_id already exists on this machine")
   139				return
   140			}
   141			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, err.Error())
   142			return
   143		}
   144	
   145		slog.Info("machine.agent.created", "machine_id", machineID, "agent_id", req.AgentID, "via", "agent_auth")
   146		writeJSON(w, http.StatusCreated, agent)
   147	}

   149	func (s *Server) handleAgentAuthUpdateAgent(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   150		hostID, ok := s.authenticateAgentToken(w, r)
   151		if !ok {
   152			return
   153		}
   154	
   155		machineID := chi.URLParam(r, "machineID")
   156		agentID := chi.URLParam(r, "agentId")
   157		if err := s.validateMachinePlacement(r.Context(), machineID, hostID); err != nil {
   158			writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, err.Error())
   159			return
   160		}
   161	
   162		existing, err := s.store.GetMachineAgent(r.Context(), machineID, agentID)
   163		if err != nil {
   164			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "agent not found")
   165			return
   166		}
   167	
   168		var req struct {
   169			Name           *string `json:"name,omitempty"`
   170			Model          *string `json:"model,omitempty"`
   171			IdentityEmoji  *string `json:"identity_emoji,omitempty"`
   172			IdentityAvatar *string `json:"identity_avatar,omitempty"`
   173			Soul           *string `json:"soul,omitempty"`
   174			IsDefault      *bool   `json:"is_default,omitempty"`
   175			SortOrder      *int    `json:"sort_order,omitempty"`
   176		}
   177		if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
   178			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid request body")
   179			return
   180		}
   181	
   182		machine, err := s.store.GetMachine(r.Context(), machineID)
   183		if err != nil {
   184			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "machine not found")
   185			return
   186		}
   187		if err := s.validateAgentModel(r.Context(), machineID, machine.AccountID, req.Model); err != nil {
   188			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, err.Error())
   189			return
   190		}
   191	
   192		if req.Name != nil {
   193			existing.Name = *req.Name
   194		}
   195		if req.Model != nil {
   196			existing.Model = req.Model
   197		}
   198		if req.IdentityEmoji != nil {
   199			existing.IdentityEmoji = req.IdentityEmoji
   200		}
   201		if req.IdentityAvatar != nil {
   202			existing.IdentityAvatar = req.IdentityAvatar
   203		}
   204		if req.Soul != nil {
   205			existing.Soul = req.Soul
   206		}
   207		if req.IsDefault != nil {
   208			existing.IsDefault = *req.IsDefault
   209		}
   210		if req.SortOrder != nil {
   211			existing.SortOrder = *req.SortOrder
   212		}
   213	
   214		if err := s.store.UpdateMachineAgent(r.Context(), existing); err != nil {
   215			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, err.Error())
   216			return
   217		}
   218	
   219		slog.Info("machine.agent.updated", "machine_id", machineID, "agent_id", agentID, "via", "agent_auth")
   220		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"status": "ok"})

   682	
   683	// reconcileHostCapacity recalculates capacity_vcpus for all active hosts
   684	// based on their physical CPU count (from capabilities) and the current
   685	// oversubscription ratio. This ensures that changing VCPU_OVERSUBSCRIPTION_RATIO
   686	// takes effect on existing hosts at startup, not only on newly created ones.
   687	func (s *Server) reconcileHostCapacity(ctx context.Context) {
   688		updated, err := s.store.ReconcileHostCapacityVCPUs(ctx, s.vcpuOversubRatio)
   689		if err != nil {
   690			slog.Error("reconcile_host_capacity.failed", "error", err)
   691			return
   692		}
   693		if updated > 0 {
   694			slog.Info("reconcile_host_capacity.updated", "hosts_updated", updated, "ratio", s.vcpuOversubRatio)
   695		}
   696	}

   700			start := time.Now()

    27		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/machines"
    28		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/provisioner"
    29		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/reconciler"
    30		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/routing"
    31		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/secrets"
    32		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/store"
    33		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/tunnel"
    34		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/internal/workflows"
    35		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/pkg/logging"
    36		"github.com/mathaix/openclawmachines/backend/pkg/version"
    37	)
    38	
    39	func main() {
    40		logging.Init("control-plane")
    41		slog.Info("server.start", "version", version.Version, "commit", version.GitCommit, "built", version.BuildTime)
    42	
    43		cfg, err := config.Load()
    44		if err != nil {
    45			slog.Error("config.load_failed", "error", err)
    46			os.Exit(1)
    47		}
    48	
    49		// Enforce strict mode: must be "api" or "worker", no combined mode.
    50		// Cloud Run runs as "api" (enqueue-only), worker fleet runs as "worker" (executor-only).
    51		if cfg.RunMode == "" {
    52			cfg.RunMode = "api" // default to API-only for Cloud Run
    53			slog.Warn("server.run_mode_defaulted", "mode", "api", "hint", "set RUN_MODE=worker for executor fleet")
    54		}
    55		if cfg.RunMode != "api" && cfg.RunMode != "worker" {
    56			slog.Error("server.invalid_run_mode", "mode", cfg.RunMode, "valid", "api, worker")
    57			os.Exit(1)
    58		}
    59		isAPI := cfg.RunMode == "api"
    60		isWorker := cfg.RunMode == "worker"
    61	
    62		ctx := context.Background()
    63	
    64		db, err := store.NewPostgresStore(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL)
    65		if err != nil {
    66			slog.Error("database.connect_failed", "error", err)
    67			os.Exit(1)
    68		}
    69		defer db.Close()
    70	
    71		// Cancellable context for background goroutines
    72		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
    73		defer cancel()
    74	
    75		// --- Worker-only mode: build server first so we can register workflows before DBOS launch ---
    76		var workerSrv *api.Server
    77		if isWorker && !isAPI {
    78			// Workers need infrastructure for migration workflows (agent, placement, KV, tunnel, machines)
    79			placementSvc := fleet.NewPlacementService(db, fleet.PlacementConfig{
    80				DefaultRegion: cfg.GCPRegion,
    81				ExpectedImage: cfg.SnapshotName,
    82				Strategy:      fleet.Spread,
    83			})
    84			agentCli := agentclient.New(cfg.AgentToken)
    85			tunnelMgr := tunnel.New(cfg.CloudflareAPIToken, cfg.CloudflareAccountID, cfg.CloudflareZoneID)
    86			kv := kvstore.New(cfg.CloudflareAccountID, cfg.CloudflareKVNamespaceID, cfg.CloudflareAPIToken)
    87	
    88			// Resolve encryption key (needed for machine secrets in migrations)
    89			encryptionKey := cfg.SecretEncryptionKey
    90			if cfg.GCPSecretName != "" {
    91				key, err := secrets.FetchSecret(ctx, cfg.GCPSecretName)
    92				if err != nil {
    93					slog.Error("config.encryption_key_fetch_failed", "error", err)
    94					os.Exit(1)
    95				}
    96				encryptionKey = key
    97			}
    98			if encryptionKey != "" && len(encryptionKey) != 32 {
    99				slog.Error("config.invalid_encryption_key", "error", "encryption key must be exactly 32 bytes", "got_bytes", len(encryptionKey))
   100				os.Exit(1)
   101			}
   102	
   103			kvAdapter := routing.NewKVAdapter(kv)
   104			routeSvc := routing.New(tunnelMgr, kvAdapter, db)
   105	
   106			machineRuntime := machines.NewRuntimeService(db, placementSvc, agentCli, tunnelMgr, nil, machines.RuntimeConfig{
   107				RootfsDataVersion:            cfg.RootfsDataVersion,
   108				CfSSHCAPubKey:                cfg.CfSSHCAPubKey,
   109				SecretKey:                    encryptionKey,
   110				ProxyBaseURL:                 cfg.ProxyBaseURL,
   111				NebiusAPIKey:                 cfg.NebiusAPIKey,
   112				OpikAPIURL:                   resolveOpikAPIURL(),
   113				ComposioAPIURL:               resolveComposioAPIURL(),
   114				EnableRuntimeVersionResolver: cfg.EnableRuntimeVersionResolver,
   115				OpenClawManifestURI:          cfg.OpenClawManifestURI,
   116			}, routeSvc)
   117	
   118			workerSrv = api.NewWorkerServer(db, placementSvc, agentCli, kv, tunnelMgr, machineRuntime, cfg.BackupMasterKey)
   119		}
   120	
   121		// --- Workflow service (both modes need this) ---
   122		// Register callback must be set before NewService when EnableRuntime=true,
   123		// because DBOS requires workflows registered before Launch.
   124		var registerFn func(dbos.DBOSContext)
   125		if workerSrv != nil {
   126			registerFn = func(dbosCtx dbos.DBOSContext) {
   127				workerSrv.RegisterWorkflows(dbosCtx)
   128			}
   129		}
   130		workflowSvc, err := workflows.NewService(ctx, db, workflows.Config{
   131			AppName:       "openclawmachines-control-plane",
   132			DatabaseURL:   cfg.DatabaseURL,
   133			EnableRuntime: cfg.EnableDurableWorkflows && isWorker,
   134			EnableEnqueue: cfg.EnableDurableWorkflows && isAPI,
   135			Register:      registerFn,
   136		})
   137		if err != nil {
   138			slog.Error("workflow.init_failed", "error", err)
   139			os.Exit(1)
   140		}
   141		defer workflowSvc.Close(10 * time.Second)
   142	
   143		// --- Worker-only mode: wire up remaining dependencies and block ---
   144		if isWorker && !isAPI {
   145			workerSrv.SetWorkflowService(workflowSvc)
   146	
   147			// Launch DBOS executor now that workflows are registered
   148			if err := workflowSvc.Launch(); err != nil {
   149				slog.Error("workflow.launch_failed", "error", err)
   150				os.Exit(1)
   151			}
   152	
   153			if cfg.ResendAPIKey != "" {
   154				workerSrv.SetEmailClient(email.NewClient(cfg.ResendAPIKey))
   155				slog.Info("email.configured", "provider", "resend")
   156			}
   157			workerSrv.SetFrontendURL(cfg.FrontendURL)
   158	
   159			slog.Info("worker.started", "executor_id", cfg.ExecutorID, "mode", "worker")
   160	
   161			// Health endpoint for MIG auto-healing
   162			healthMux := http.NewServeMux()
   163			healthMux.HandleFunc("/healthz", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   164				if err := db.Ping(r.Context()); err != nil {
   165					w.WriteHeader(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
   166					_, _ = w.Write([]byte("db unhealthy"))
   167					return
   168				}
   169				w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
   170				_, _ = w.Write([]byte("ok"))
   171			})
   172			healthServer := &http.Server{Addr: ":" + cfg.Port, Handler: healthMux}
   173			go func() { _ = healthServer.ListenAndServe() }()
   174	
   175			// Signal handling + preemption
   176			sigCh := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
   177			signal.Notify(sigCh, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
   178			go watchForPreemption(sigCh)
   179	
   180			<-sigCh
   181			slog.Info("worker.shutdown_start")
   182			cancel()
   183			_ = healthServer.Shutdown(context.Background())
   184			return
   185		}
   186	
   187		// --- API mode (or both modes): full infrastructure ---
   188	
   189		// Auth setup based on AUTH_MODE
   190		var a *auth.Auth
   191		var cfAuth *auth.CfAccessAuth
   192		var firebaseAuth *auth.FirebaseAuth
   193		switch cfg.AuthMode {
   194		case "dev":
   195			slog.Warn("auth.dev_mode_enabled", "email", cfg.DevUserEmail)
   196			if cfg.JWTSecret != "" && len(cfg.JWTSecret) >= 16 {
   197				a = auth.New(cfg.JWTSecret)
   198			}
   199		case "cfaccess":
   200			if cfg.CfAccessTeamDomain == "" || cfg.CfAccessAUD == "" {
   201				slog.Error("cfaccess.config_missing", "error", "CF_ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN and CF_ACCESS_AUD required for cfaccess mode")
   202				os.Exit(1)
   203			}
   204			cfAuth = auth.NewCfAccess(cfg.CfAccessTeamDomain, cfg.CfAccessAUD)
   205			if cfg.JWTSecret != "" && len(cfg.JWTSecret) >= 16 {
   206				a = auth.New(cfg.JWTSecret) // for dual-mode fallback
   207			}
   208		case "firebase":
   209			if cfg.FirebaseProjectID == "" {
   210				slog.Error("firebase.config_missing", "error", "FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID required for firebase mode")
   211				os.Exit(1)
   212			}
   213			firebaseAuth = auth.NewFirebaseAuth(cfg.FirebaseProjectID)
   214			if cfg.JWTSecret != "" && len(cfg.JWTSecret) >= 16 {
   215				a = auth.New(cfg.JWTSecret)
   216			}
   217			slog.Info("auth.firebase_enabled", "project_id", cfg.FirebaseProjectID)
   218		default:
   219			slog.Error("auth.unknown_mode", "mode", cfg.AuthMode)
   220			os.Exit(1)
   221		}
   222	
   223		placementSvc := fleet.NewPlacementService(db, fleet.PlacementConfig{
   224			DefaultRegion: cfg.GCPRegion,
   225			ExpectedImage: cfg.SnapshotName,
   226			Strategy:      fleet.Spread,
   227		})
   228		agentCli := agentclient.New(cfg.AgentToken)
   229		tunnelMgr := tunnel.New(cfg.CloudflareAPIToken, cfg.CloudflareAccountID, cfg.CloudflareZoneID)
   230		if tunnelMgr == nil {
   231			slog.Error("tunnel.not_configured", "error", "CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN, CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID, CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID required")
   232			os.Exit(1)
   233		}
   234		prov := provisioner.New(provisioner.Config{
   235			Store:                    db,
   236			AgentClient:              agentCli,
   237			Tunnel:                   tunnelMgr,
   238			Project:                  cfg.GCPProject,
   239			Zone:                     cfg.GCPZone,
   240			Region:                   cfg.GCPRegion,
   241			Snapshot:                 cfg.SnapshotName,
   242			AgentToken:               cfg.AgentToken,
   243			BackendURL:               cfg.BackendURL,
   244			DataDiskSizeGB:           cfg.DataDiskSizeGB,
   245			RootfsGCSManifest:        cfg.RootfsGCSManifest,
   246			AgentGCSManifest:         cfg.AgentGCSManifest,
   247			BrowserRootfsGCSManifest: cfg.BrowserRootfsGCSManifest,
   248			BackupMasterKey:          cfg.BackupMasterKey,
   249		})
   250		// Resolve encryption key: GCP Secret Manager > env var > fatal
   251		encryptionKey := cfg.SecretEncryptionKey
   252		if cfg.GCPSecretName != "" {
   253			slog.Info("config.fetching_encryption_key", "source", "gcp_secret_manager", "secret_name", cfg.GCPSecretName)
   254			key, err := secrets.FetchSecret(ctx, cfg.GCPSecretName)
   255			if err != nil {
   256				slog.Error("config.encryption_key_fetch_failed", "error", err)
   257				os.Exit(1)
   258			}
   259			encryptionKey = key
   260		} else if encryptionKey == "" {
   261			slog.Error("config.missing_encryption_key", "error", "no encryption key configured (set GCP_SECRET_NAME or SECRET_ENCRYPTION_KEY)")
   262			os.Exit(1)
   263		}
   264		if len(encryptionKey) != 32 {
   265			slog.Error("config.invalid_encryption_key", "error", "encryption key must be exactly 32 bytes", "got_bytes", len(encryptionKey))
   266			os.Exit(1)
   267		}
   268		cfg.SecretEncryptionKey = encryptionKey
   269	
   270		kv := kvstore.New(cfg.CloudflareAccountID, cfg.CloudflareKVNamespaceID, cfg.CloudflareAPIToken)
   271		if kv == nil {
   272			slog.Error("kv.not_configured", "error", "CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID, CLOUDFLARE_KV_NAMESPACE_ID, CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN required")
   273			os.Exit(1)
   274		}
   275	
   276		kvAdapter := routing.NewKVAdapter(kv)
   277		routeSvc := routing.New(tunnelMgr, kvAdapter, db)
   278	
   279		// Start route projector (DB → KV sync every 60s)
   280		routeReader := routing.NewStoreAdapter(db)
   281		projector := routing.NewProjector(routeReader, kvAdapter)
   282		go projector.Start(ctx, 60*time.Second)
   283	
   284		machineRuntime := machines.NewRuntimeService(db, placementSvc, agentCli, tunnelMgr, nil, machines.RuntimeConfig{
   285			RootfsDataVersion:            cfg.RootfsDataVersion,
   286			CfSSHCAPubKey:                cfg.CfSSHCAPubKey,
   287			SecretKey:                    cfg.SecretEncryptionKey,
   288			ProxyBaseURL:                 cfg.ProxyBaseURL,
   289			NebiusAPIKey:                 cfg.NebiusAPIKey,
   290			OpikAPIURL:                   resolveOpikAPIURL(),
   291			ComposioAPIURL:               resolveComposioAPIURL(),
   292			EnableRuntimeVersionResolver: cfg.EnableRuntimeVersionResolver,
   293			OpenClawManifestURI:          cfg.OpenClawManifestURI,
   294		}, routeSvc)
   295	
   296		srv := api.NewServer(ctx, db, a, cfg.AuthMode, cfAuth, firebaseAuth, placementSvc, agentCli, prov, tunnelMgr, cfg.CORSOrigins, cfg.SecretEncryptionKey, kv, cfg.CFServiceTokenID, cfg.CFServiceTokenSecret, cfg.AgentToken, cfg.RootfsDataVersion, cfg.DevUserEmail, cfg.CfSSHCAPubKey, cfg.ProxyBaseURL, cfg.OAuthClientID, cfg.OAuthClientSecret, machineRuntime, cfg.BackendURL, cfg.RootfsGCSManifest, cfg.AgentGCSManifest, cfg.GCSServiceAccountKey, cfg.BackupMasterKey, cfg.BackupGCSBucket, cfg.BackupGCSPrefix)
   297	
   298		srv.SetRouting(routeSvc)
   299	
   300		if key := os.Getenv("COMPOSIO_CONSUMER_KEY"); key != "" {
   301			srv.SetComposioClient(composio.NewClient("https://backend.composio.dev", key))
   302			slog.Info("composio.configured")
   303		}
   304		srv.SetComposioAPIURL(resolveComposioAPIURL())
   305	
   306		// Register workflows then launch executor (order matters: register before launch)
   307		workflowSvc.RegisterWorkflows(srv.RegisterWorkflows)
   308		if err := workflowSvc.Launch(); err != nil {
   309			slog.Error("workflow.launch_failed", "error", err)
   310			os.Exit(1)
   311		}
   312	
   313		srv.SetWorkflowService(workflowSvc)
   314	
   315		// Activity log for business-level event tracking
   316		activityResolver := events.NewStoreResolver(db)
   317		activityLogger := events.New(db, activityResolver)
   318		srv.SetActivity(activityLogger)
   319		prov.SetActivity(activityLogger)
   320		slog.Info("activity.configured")
   321	
   322		if cfg.ResendAPIKey != "" {
   323			srv.SetEmailClient(email.NewClient(cfg.ResendAPIKey))
   324			slog.Info("email.configured", "provider", "resend")
   325		}
   326		srv.SetFrontendURL(cfg.FrontendURL)
   327		srv.SetDataPlaneDomain(cfg.DataPlaneDomain)
   328		srv.SetCfAccessAuthDomain(cfg.CfAccessTeamDomain)
   329		if cfg.SSHCAPrivateKey != "" {
   330			srv.SetSSHCAPrivateKey(cfg.SSHCAPrivateKey)
   331			slog.Info("ssh_ca.configured")
   332		}
   333	
   334		// OAuth refresh is now handled inline by the proxy (no background loop).
   335		srv.StartInvitationExpiryLoop(ctx)
   336		srv.StartStallDetectionLoop(ctx)
   337		srv.StartBrowseSessionJanitor(ctx)
   338	
   339		computeClient, err := compute.NewInstancesRESTClient(ctx)
   340		if err != nil {
   341			slog.Error("reconciler.compute_client.failed", "error", err)
   342		} else {
   343			instanceChecker := reconciler.NewGCPInstanceChecker(computeClient)
   344			hostReconciler := reconciler.New(db, instanceChecker, machineRuntime, cfg.GCPProject, 180*time.Second)
   345			hostReconciler.SetActivity(activityLogger)
   346			go hostReconciler.Start(ctx, 60*time.Second)
   347			slog.Info("reconciler.started")
   348		}
   349	
   350		httpServer := &http.Server{
   351			Addr:    ":" + cfg.Port,
   352			Handler: srv,
   353		}
   354	
   355		go func() {
   356			sigCh := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
   357			signal.Notify(sigCh, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
   358			<-sigCh
   359			slog.Info("server.shutdown_start")
   360			cancel()

    11		Port                         string
    12		DatabaseURL                  string
    13		JWTSecret                    string
    14		AgentToken                   string

    16		GCPProject                   string

    27		CFServiceTokenSecret         string
    28		CloudflareZoneID             string

    30		DataDiskSizeGB               int64

    52		NebiusAPIKey                 string // platform Nebius Token Factory key

    54		DataPlaneDomain              string // domain for data-plane hostnames (e.g. "openclawmachines.com")
    55		SSHCAPrivateKey              string // platform SSH CA private key (signs SSH certs) (pushed to VMs)
    56		EnableRuntimeVersionResolver bool
    57		OpenClawManifestURI          string
    58	}

    60	func Load() (*Config, error) {
    61		zone := getEnv("GCP_ZONE", "us-central1-b")
    62	
    63		// Derive region from zone by stripping the last "-X" suffix (e.g. "us-central1-b" → "us-central1")
    64		region := zone
    65		if idx := len(zone) - 2; idx > 0 && zone[idx] == '-' {
    66			region = zone[:idx]
    67		}
    68	
    69		cfg := &Config{
    70			Port:                    getEnv("PORT", "8080"),
    71			DatabaseURL:             os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL"),
    72			JWTSecret:               os.Getenv("JWT_SECRET"),
    73			AgentToken:              os.Getenv("FC_AGENT_TOKEN"),
    74			CORSOrigins:             getEnv("CORS_ORIGINS", "http://localhost:5173"),
    75			GCPProject:              getEnv("GCP_PROJECT", "clarateach"),
    76			GCPZone:                 zone,
    77			GCPRegion:               region,
    78			LiteLLMMasterKey:        os.Getenv("LITELLM_MASTER_KEY"),
    79			BackendURL:              os.Getenv("BACKEND_URL"),
    80			SnapshotName:            getEnv("FC_SNAPSHOT_NAME", os.Getenv("OCM_SNAPSHOT")),
    81			SecretEncryptionKey:     os.Getenv("SECRET_ENCRYPTION_KEY"),
    82			CloudflareAccountID:     os.Getenv("CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"),
    83			CloudflareKVNamespaceID: os.Getenv("CLOUDFLARE_KV_NAMESPACE_ID"),
    84			CloudflareAPIToken:      os.Getenv("CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN"),
    85			CFServiceTokenID:        os.Getenv("CF_SERVICE_TOKEN_ID"),
    86			CFServiceTokenSecret:    os.Getenv("CF_SERVICE_TOKEN_SECRET"),
    87			CloudflareZoneID:        os.Getenv("CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID"),
    88			GCPSecretName:           os.Getenv("GCP_SECRET_NAME"),
    89		}
    90	
    91		cfg.DataDiskSizeGB = int64(getEnvInt("DATA_DISK_SIZE_GB", 50))
    92		cfg.RootfsGCSManifest = os.Getenv("ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST")
    93		cfg.AgentGCSManifest = os.Getenv("AGENT_GCS_MANIFEST")
    94		// Preserve the "empty means disabled" contract: explicit
    95		// BROWSER_ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST="" keeps browser VM support off,
    96		// while an unset env falls back to the default manifest. Using
    97		// LookupEnv rather than getEnv (which collapses both to fallback).
    98		cfg.BrowserRootfsGCSManifest = getEnvOrDefault("BROWSER_ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST", "gs://openclawmachines/kernel-browser-rootfs/manifest.json")
    99		cfg.RootfsDataVersion = getEnvInt("ROOTFS_DATA_VERSION", 1)
   100	
   101		cfg.AuthMode = getEnv("AUTH_MODE", "cfaccess")
   102		cfg.CfAccessTeamDomain = strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("CF_ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN"))
   103		cfg.CfAccessAUD = strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("CF_ACCESS_AUD"))
   104		cfg.DevUserEmail = getEnv("DEV_USER_EMAIL", "dev@localhost")
   105		cfg.CfSSHCAPubKey = os.Getenv("CF_SSH_CA_PUBKEY")
   106		cfg.ProxyBaseURL = getEnv("OCM_PROXY_BASE_URL", "http://192.168.100.1:4000")
   107		cfg.OAuthClientID = os.Getenv("OAUTH_CLIENT_ID")
   108		cfg.OAuthClientSecret = os.Getenv("OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET")
   109		cfg.GCSServiceAccountKey = os.Getenv("GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY")
   110		cfg.BackupMasterKey = os.Getenv("BACKUP_MASTER_KEY")
   111		cfg.BackupGCSBucket = getEnv("BACKUP_GCS_BUCKET", "openclawmachines")
   112		cfg.BackupGCSPrefix = getEnv("BACKUP_GCS_PREFIX", "backups")
   113		cfg.EnableDurableWorkflows = getEnv("ENABLE_DURABLE_WORKFLOWS", "") == "1"
   114		cfg.RunMode = getEnv("RUN_MODE", "")
   115		cfg.ResendAPIKey = os.Getenv("RESEND_API_KEY")
   116		cfg.FrontendURL = getEnv("FRONTEND_URL", "http://localhost:5173")
   117		cfg.ExecutorID = getEnv("EXECUTOR_ID", "")
   118		cfg.NebiusAPIKey = os.Getenv("NEBIUS_API_KEY")
   119		cfg.FirebaseProjectID = os.Getenv("FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID")
   120		cfg.DataPlaneDomain = getEnv("DATA_PLANE_DOMAIN", "openclawmachines.com")
   121		cfg.SSHCAPrivateKey = os.Getenv("OCM_SSH_CA_PRIVATE_KEY")
   122		cfg.EnableRuntimeVersionResolver = getEnv("FF_RUNTIME_VERSION_RESOLVER", "") == "1"
   123		cfg.OpenClawManifestURI = os.Getenv("OPENCLAW_GCS_MANIFEST")
   124		if cfg.ExecutorID == "" {
   125			hostname, _ := os.Hostname()
   126			cfg.ExecutorID = hostname
   127		}
   128	
   129		if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
   130			return nil, fmt.Errorf("DATABASE_URL is required")
   131		}
   132	
   133		return cfg, nil
   134	}

   136	// AgentConfig is the config for the Worker Agent process.
   137	type AgentConfig struct {
   138		ControlPort string
   139		ProxyPort   string
   140		AgentToken  string
   141	
   142		// Network
   143		BridgeName string
   144		BridgeIP   string
   145	
   146		// Paths
   147		ImagesDir           string
   148		StateDir            string
   149		SocketDir           string
   150		DataDir             string
   151		RuntimeStateDir     string
   152		OpenClawRuntimeDir  string
   153		OpenClawManifestURI string
   154		KernelPath          string
   155	
   156		// VM defaults
   157		VCPUs    int
   158		MemoryMB int
   159	
   160		// Backend connection
   161		BackendURL string
   162		HostID     string // host DB ID (from GCP metadata)
   163	
   164		// Cloudflare Tunnel token (prefetched from GCP metadata)
   165		TunnelToken string
   166	
   167		// CIDR allowlist for the control API (comma-separated, empty = allow all)
   168		ControlAllowedCIDRs string
   169	
   170		// GCS rootfs distribution (empty = use embedded rootfs from boot disk)
   171		RootfsGCSManifest     string // ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST - GCS URI
   172		RootfsDownloadTimeout int    // ROOTFS_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT - seconds (default 600)
   173		RootfsRetryAttempts   int    // ROOTFS_RETRY_ATTEMPTS (default 3)
   174	
   175		// GCS browser rootfs distribution (empty = no browser companion VM support)
   176		BrowserRootfsGCSManifest string // BROWSER_ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST - GCS URI
   177	
   178		// OpenClaw runtime artifact distribution (empty = local cache only)
   179		OpenClawDownloadTimeout int // OPENCLAW_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT - seconds (default 300)
   180		OpenClawRetryAttempts   int // OPENCLAW_RETRY_ATTEMPTS (default 3)
   181	
   182		// VM runtime owner (default "direct", optional "systemd-unit")
   183		RuntimeOwnerKind string // VM_RUNTIME_OWNER
   184	
   185		// Agent self-update (empty = disabled). Updates are driven by the
   186		// startup check and the admin-triggered /trigger-update endpoint —
   187		// there is no background polling.
   188		AgentGCSManifest string // AGENT_GCS_MANIFEST - GCS URI for agent manifest
   189	
   190		// Agent endpoint (reported in heartbeat so control plane knows how to reach us)
   191		AgentEndpoint string // AGENT_ENDPOINT - e.g. "http://203.0.113.10:9090"
   192	
   193		// Backup
   194		BackupGCSBucket      string // BACKUP_GCS_BUCKET (default "openclawmachines")
   195		BackupGCSPrefix      string // BACKUP_GCS_PREFIX (default "backups")
   196		BackupMaxVolumeGB    int    // BACKUP_MAX_VOLUME_GB (default 10)
   197		BackupMasterKey      string // BACKUP_MASTER_KEY - 32-byte hex-encoded platform master key
   198		GCSServiceAccountKey string // GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY - JSON key for non-GCP hosts
   199	}

   247	func getEnv(key, fallback string) string {
   248		if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
   249			return v
   250		}
   251		return fallback
   252	}

   254	// getEnvOrDefault returns the env var's value when the var is SET (even to
   255	// an empty string), or the fallback when the var is unset. It differs from
   256	// getEnv by preserving explicit empty values as a meaningful signal. Used
   257	// for BROWSER_ROOTFS_GCS_MANIFEST where "" means "browser VM support is
   258	// disabled" and must not be silently overwritten with the default.

   260		if v, ok := os.LookupEnv(key); ok {

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba backend/migrations/072_browser_vms.sql | sed -n '1,140p'" in /home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	-- browser_vms table
     2	--
     3	-- ON DELETE semantics:
     4	--   - account_id: CASCADE so deleting an account drops its browser VM records
     5	--     instead of blocking the account teardown on dangling rows.
     6	--   - host_id: SET NULL so retiring a host clears the pointer rather than
     7	--     blocking host decommissioning. The agent-side VM is already gone at
     8	--     that point; the DB just needs to stop pointing at the missing host.
     9	CREATE TABLE browser_vms (
    10	    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    11	    account_id      INT NOT NULL REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    12	    slug            TEXT NOT NULL,
    13	    name            TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    14	    host_id         INT REFERENCES hosts(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    15	    vm_ip           TEXT,
    16	    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'stopped',
    17	    vcpus           INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    18	    memory_mb       INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1024,
    19	    cdp_port        INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 9222,
    20	    rootfs_version  TEXT,
    21	    error_message   TEXT,
    22	    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    23	    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    24	    CONSTRAINT browser_vms_status_check CHECK (status IN ('stopped', 'provisioning', 'running', 'error')),
    25	    UNIQUE(account_id, slug)
    26	);
    27	
    28	CREATE INDEX idx_browser_vms_account ON browser_vms(account_id);
    29	CREATE INDEX idx_browser_vms_host ON browser_vms(host_id) WHERE host_id IS NOT NULL;
    30	
    31	-- browser_vm_placements table
    32	--
    33	-- host_id CASCADE: placements are ephemeral capacity accounting, so
    34	-- decommissioning a host should drop their placement history rather than
    35	-- block the host deletion. browser_vm_id is already CASCADE for the same
    36	-- reason — the placement has no meaning without its owning browser VM.
    37	CREATE TABLE browser_vm_placements (
    38	    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    39	    browser_vm_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES browser_vms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    40	    host_id         INT NOT NULL REFERENCES hosts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    41	    vm_ip           TEXT,
    42	    state           TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reserved',
    43	    reserved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    44	    activated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    45	    released_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    46	    CONSTRAINT browser_vm_placements_state_check CHECK (state IN ('reserved', 'active', 'released'))
    47	);
    48	
    49	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_bvp_active_browser_vm
    50	ON browser_vm_placements(browser_vm_id) WHERE released_at IS NULL;
    51	
    52	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_bvp_active_host_ip
    53	ON browser_vm_placements(host_id, vm_ip) WHERE released_at IS NULL;
    54	
    55	CREATE INDEX idx_bvp_host
    56	ON browser_vm_placements(host_id) WHERE released_at IS NULL;
    57	
    58	-- pairing column on machines
    59	--
    60	-- ON DELETE SET NULL: deleting a browser VM must not be blocked by a stale
    61	-- pairing pointer on a machine row. handleDeleteBrowserVM runs the agent-side
    62	-- unpair first (best effort), and this constraint guarantees the DB delete
    63	-- succeeds even if the agent unpair failed and left the pointer in place.
    64	ALTER TABLE machines ADD COLUMN browser_vm_id UUID REFERENCES browser_vms(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
    65	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_machines_browser_vm ON machines(browser_vm_id) WHERE browser_vm_id IS NOT NULL;

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba backend/migrations/073_browser_vm_fk_actions.sql | sed -n '1,90p'" in /home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	-- Forward migration for the ON DELETE actions backfilled into 072.
     2	--
     3	-- Databases that applied 072 before the branch-review fix landed still carry
     4	-- the original FK actions (NO ACTION everywhere), so the cleanup paths in
     5	-- handleDeleteBrowserVM / account teardown / host decommissioning would still
     6	-- fail with FK violations. This migration brings those dbs in line with the
     7	-- edited 072 file without re-running it.
     8	--
     9	-- Each constraint is dropped and re-created so the behavior change is
    10	-- explicit and auditable. Postgres auto-names FK constraints as
    11	-- <table>_<column>_fkey; we drop by that name and recreate with the
    12	-- desired action.
    13	
    14	BEGIN;
    15	
    16	-- machines.browser_vm_id → ON DELETE SET NULL
    17	-- Critical: without this, DeleteBrowserVM hits FK when
    18	-- cleanupBrowserPairing's agent-side unpair failed and left the pairing
    19	-- pointer in place.
    20	ALTER TABLE machines
    21	    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS machines_browser_vm_id_fkey;
    22	ALTER TABLE machines
    23	    ADD CONSTRAINT machines_browser_vm_id_fkey
    24	    FOREIGN KEY (browser_vm_id) REFERENCES browser_vms(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
    25	
    26	-- browser_vms.account_id → ON DELETE CASCADE
    27	ALTER TABLE browser_vms
    28	    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS browser_vms_account_id_fkey;
    29	ALTER TABLE browser_vms
    30	    ADD CONSTRAINT browser_vms_account_id_fkey
    31	    FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
    32	
    33	-- browser_vms.host_id → ON DELETE SET NULL
    34	ALTER TABLE browser_vms
    35	    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS browser_vms_host_id_fkey;
    36	ALTER TABLE browser_vms
    37	    ADD CONSTRAINT browser_vms_host_id_fkey
    38	    FOREIGN KEY (host_id) REFERENCES hosts(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
    39	
    40	-- browser_vm_placements.host_id → ON DELETE CASCADE
    41	ALTER TABLE browser_vm_placements
    42	    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS browser_vm_placements_host_id_fkey;
    43	ALTER TABLE browser_vm_placements
    44	    ADD CONSTRAINT browser_vm_placements_host_id_fkey
    45	    FOREIGN KEY (host_id) REFERENCES hosts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
    46	
    47	COMMIT;

    17	type PostgresStore struct {
    18		pool *pgxpool.Pool
    19	}
    20	
    21	func NewPostgresStore(ctx context.Context, databaseURL string) (*PostgresStore, error) {
    22		pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, databaseURL)
    23		if err != nil {
    24			return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
    25		}
    26	
    27		if err := pool.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
    28			return nil, fmt.Errorf("ping database: %w", err)
    29		}
    30	
    31		return &PostgresStore{pool: pool}, nil
    32	}
    33	
    34	func (s *PostgresStore) Close() {
    35		s.pool.Close()
    36	}
    37	
    38	func (s *PostgresStore) Ping(ctx context.Context) error {
    39		return s.pool.Ping(ctx)
    40	}
    41	
    42	// ---- Users ----
    43	
    44	func (s *PostgresStore) CreateUser(ctx context.Context, user *User) error {
    45		return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    46			`INSERT INTO users (email, name, avatar_url, auth_provider, auth_provider_id, cf_sub, identity_issuer, identity_subject, status)
    47			 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, NULLIF($6, ''), $7, $8, COALESCE(NULLIF($9, ''), 'active'))
    48			 RETURNING id, created_at`,
    49			user.Email, user.Name, user.AvatarURL, user.AuthProvider, user.AuthProviderID,
    50			user.CfSub, user.IdentityIssuer, user.IdentitySubject, user.Status,
    51		).Scan(&user.ID, &user.CreatedAt)
    52	}
    53	
    54	func (s *PostgresStore) GetUser(ctx context.Context, id int) (*User, error) {
    55		u := &User{}
    56		err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    57			`SELECT id, email, name, avatar_url, auth_provider, auth_provider_id, COALESCE(cf_sub, ''), identity_issuer, identity_subject, status, created_at
    58			 FROM users WHERE id = $1`, id,
    59		).Scan(&u.ID, &u.Email, &u.Name, &u.AvatarURL, &u.AuthProvider, &u.AuthProviderID, &u.CfSub, &u.IdentityIssuer, &u.IdentitySubject, &u.Status, &u.CreatedAt)
    60		if err != nil {
    61			return nil, err
    62		}
    63		return u, nil
    64	}

   125			userID, provider, providerID, avatarURL)

   140	func (s *PostgresStore) CreateAccountWithOwner(ctx context.Context, a *Account, ownerUserID int) error {
   141		tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   142		if err != nil {
   143			return fmt.Errorf("begin transaction: %w", err)
   144		}
   145		defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
   146	
   147		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   148			`INSERT INTO accounts (name, slug, plan, billing_email, created_by)
   149			 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
   150			 RETURNING id, created_at`,
   151			a.Name, a.Slug, a.Plan, a.BillingEmail, a.CreatedBy,
   152		).Scan(&a.ID, &a.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   153			return err
   154		}
   155	
   156		var memberCreatedAt time.Time
   157		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   158			`INSERT INTO account_members (account_id, user_id, role)
   159			 VALUES ($1, $2, 'owner')
   160			 RETURNING created_at`,
   161			a.ID, ownerUserID,
   162		).Scan(&memberCreatedAt); err != nil {
   163			return fmt.Errorf("create owner membership: %w", err)
   164		}
   165	
   166		return tx.Commit(ctx)
   167	}
   168	
   169	func (s *PostgresStore) UpdateAccountName(ctx context.Context, accountID int, name string) error {
   170		ct, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,
   171			`UPDATE accounts SET name = $1 WHERE id = $2`, name, accountID)
   172		if err != nil {
   173			return err
   174		}
   175		if ct.RowsAffected() == 0 {
   176			return fmt.Errorf("account not found")
   177		}
   178		return nil
   179	}

   205	func (s *PostgresStore) ListAccountsByUser(ctx context.Context, userID int) ([]Account, error) {
   206		rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   207			`SELECT a.id, a.name, a.slug, a.plan, a.billing_email, a.stripe_customer_id, a.created_by, a.created_at
   208			 FROM accounts a

   222			}

   249			 FROM account_members am
   250			 JOIN users u ON u.id = am.user_id
   251			 WHERE am.account_id = $1 ORDER BY am.created_at`, accountID)

   261				return nil, err
   262			}
   263			members = append(members, m)
   264		}
   265		return members, nil
