  1085	
  1086	// UpdateHostHeartbeat atomically updates the host's last_heartbeat, external_ip,
  1087	// and agent_version in a single statement. Returns ipChanged=true if the IP was
  1088	// different from what was previously stored.
  1089	//
  1090	// Uses a CTE to snapshot the old IP before the UPDATE so the comparison is
  1091	// accurate (RETURNING runs after SET, so comparing in RETURNING would always
  1092	// see the new value).
  1093	func (s *PostgresStore) UpdateHostHeartbeat(ctx context.Context, id int, externalIP, agentVersion, rootfsVersion, browserRootfsVersion, openclawVersion string) (bool, error) {
  1094		var ipChanged bool
  1095		err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
  1096			`WITH old AS (
  1097			   SELECT external_ip FROM hosts WHERE id = $1
  1098			 )
  1099			 UPDATE hosts
  1100			 SET last_heartbeat        = now(),
  1101			     external_ip           = $2,
  1102			     agent_version         = $3,
  1103			     rootfs_version        = $4,
  1104			     browser_rootfs_version = $5,
  1105			     openclaw_version       = CASE WHEN $6 = '' THEN openclaw_version ELSE $6 END
  1106			 FROM old
  1107			 WHERE hosts.id = $1
  1108			 RETURNING (old.external_ip IS DISTINCT FROM $2)`,
  1109			id, externalIP, agentVersion, rootfsVersion, browserRootfsVersion, openclawVersion,
  1110		).Scan(&ipChanged)
  1111		return ipChanged, err
  1112	}
  1113	
  1114	func (s *PostgresStore) ListStaleHosts(ctx context.Context, threshold time.Duration) ([]Host, error) {
  1115		rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
  1116			`SELECT id, vm_name, vm_id, zone, region, machine_type, external_ip, internal_ip,
  1117			        tunnel_url, status, status_message, source_image, capacity_vcpus, capacity_memory_mb, capacity_disk_mb,
  1118			        used_vcpus, used_memory_mb, used_disk_mb, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM machines WHERE machines.host_id = hosts.id AND machines.status = 'running') AS machine_count, agent_version, openclaw_version,
  1119			        rootfs_version, browser_rootfs_version, last_heartbeat, created_at,
  1120			        provider, provider_class, lifecycle_mode, agent_endpoint, agent_endpoint_type,
  1121			        provider_host_id, provider_metadata, capabilities, labels, agent_token, maintenance_mode
  1122			 FROM hosts
  1123			 WHERE status = 'ready'
  1124			   AND maintenance_mode = false
  1125			   AND (last_heartbeat IS NULL OR last_heartbeat < now() - $1::interval)`, threshold.String())

    52	  const [showDeleteConfirm, setShowDeleteConfirm] = useState(false);
    53	  const pollRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
    54	  const { toast } = useToast();
    55	  const [openclawReleases, setOpenclawReleases] = useState<{ version: string; exact_version: string }[]>([]);
    56	  const [rootfsReleases, setRootfsReleases] = useState<{ version: string; exact_version: string }[]>([]);
    57	  const latestRelease = openclawReleases[0] ?? null;
    58	  const latestRootfsRelease = rootfsReleases[0] ?? null;
    59	  const [upgrading, setUpgrading] = useState(false);
    60	  const [upgradeDismissed, setUpgradeDismissed] = useState(false);

    67	  };

    69	  const fetchMachine = useCallback(async () => {
    70	    if (!id || !accountId) return;
    71	    try {
    72	      const data = await getMachine(accountId, id);
    73	      setMachine(data);
    74	    } catch (err) {
    75	      setFetchError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load machine");
    76	    } finally {
    77	      setLoading(false);
    78	    }
    79	  }, [id, accountId]);
    80	
    81	  // Initial fetch
    82	  useEffect(() => {
    83	    fetchMachine();
    84	  }, [fetchMachine]);
    85	
    86	  // Fetch stable release history for openclaw + rootfs. The list is kept (not just
    87	  // the first element) so the banner can tell the difference between "running an
    88	  // older stable" and "running a newer non-stable (RC/dev)" — the latter shouldn't
    89	  // offer a downgrade disguised as an upgrade.
    90	  useEffect(() => {
    91	    if (!accountId) return;
    92	    Promise.all([
    93	      listOpenClawReleases(accountId).catch(() => [] as { version: string; exact_version: string }[]),
    94	      listRootfsReleases(accountId).catch(() => [] as { version: string; exact_version: string }[]),
    95	    ]).then(([openclaw, rootfs]) => {
    96	      setOpenclawReleases(openclaw);
    97	      setRootfsReleases(rootfs);
    98	    });
    99	  }, [accountId]);
   100	
   101	  // Poll while booting (fallback if SSE disconnects)
   102	  const isBooting = machine?.status === "provisioning" || machine?.status === "starting";
   103	  useEffect(() => {
   104	    if (pollRef.current) {
   105	      clearInterval(pollRef.current);
   106	      pollRef.current = null;
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    if (isBooting) {
   110	      pollRef.current = setInterval(fetchMachine, 5000);
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    return () => {
   114	      if (pollRef.current) {
   115	        clearInterval(pollRef.current);
   116	      }
   117	    };
   118	  }, [machine?.status, fetchMachine, isBooting]);
   119	
   120	  const handleStart = async () => {
   121	    if (!id || !accountId) return;
   122	    setActing("starting");
   123	    try {
   124	      await startMachine(accountId, id);
   125	      await fetchMachine();
   126	      toast({ title: "Machine starting", variant: "success" });
   127	    } catch (err) {
   128	      toast({ title: "Failed to start", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to start", variant: "error" });
   129	    } finally {
   130	      setActing("");
   131	    }
   132	  };
   133	
   134	  const handleStop = async () => {
   135	    if (!id || !accountId) return;
   136	    setActing("stopping");
   137	    try {
   138	      await stopMachine(accountId, id);
   139	      await fetchMachine();
   140	      toast({ title: "Machine stopped", variant: "success" });
   141	    } catch (err) {
   142	      toast({ title: "Failed to stop", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to stop", variant: "error" });
   143	    } finally {
   144	      setActing("");
   145	    }
   146	  };
   147	
   148	  const handleDelete = async () => {
   149	    if (!id || !accountId) return;
   150	    setActing("deleting");
   151	    try {
   152	      await deleteMachine(accountId, id);
   153	      toast({ title: "Machine deleted", variant: "success" });
   154	      navigate("/dashboard");
   155	    } catch (err) {
   156	      toast({ title: "Failed to delete", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to delete", variant: "error" });
   157	      setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
   158	    } finally {
   159	      setActing("");
   160	    }
   161	  };
   162	
   163	  const handleUpgrade = async () => {
   164	    if (!id || !accountId || !latestRelease || !latestRootfsRelease) return;
   165	    setUpgrading(true);
   166	    try {
   167	      // Stop the machine first if it's running — the new versions take effect on next start.
   168	      if (machine?.status === "running") {
   169	        toast({ title: "Stopping machine for upgrade...", variant: "success" });
   170	        await stopMachine(accountId, id);
   171	        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
   172	      }
   173	      const resp = await upgradeMachineRuntime(
   174	        accountId,
   175	        id,
   176	        latestRootfsRelease.exact_version,
   177	        latestRelease.exact_version,
   178	      );
   179	      if (resp.error) {
   180	        toast({ title: "Upgrade failed", description: resp.error, variant: "error" });
   181	      } else {
   182	        setUpgradeDismissed(true);
   183	        toast({
   184	          title: "Upgrade queued",
   185	          description: "Start the machine to use the new versions",
   186	          variant: "success",
   187	        });
   188	        await fetchMachine();
   189	      }
   190	    } catch (err) {
   191	      toast({ title: "Upgrade failed", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Request failed", variant: "error" });
   192	    } finally {
   193	      setUpgrading(false);
   194	    }
   195	  };
   196	
   197	  // "Behind latest stable" is only true when the current version appears in the
   198	  // stable list at index > 0 (it IS stable, just an older one). If current isn't
   199	  // in the list at all, it's an RC/dev/one-off build — offering to "upgrade" to
   200	  // the latest stable would be a downgrade, so don't show the banner in that case.
   201	  const isBehindLatestStable = (current: string, list: { exact_version: string }[]): boolean => {
   202	    if (!current || list.length === 0) return false;
   203	    const idx = list.findIndex((r) => r.exact_version === current);
   204	    return idx > 0;
   205	  };
   206	
   207	  const currentOpenclaw = machine?.actual_openclaw_version || machine?.resolved_openclaw_version || "";
   208	  const desiredOpenclaw = machine?.desired_openclaw_version || "";
   209	  const currentRootfs = machine?.actual_rootfs_version || machine?.resolved_rootfs_version || "";
   210	  const desiredRootfs = machine?.desired_rootfs_version || "";
   211	  const openclawAlreadyTargetingLatest = !!(latestRelease && desiredOpenclaw && latestRelease.exact_version === desiredOpenclaw);
   212	  const rootfsAlreadyTargetingLatest = !!(latestRootfsRelease && desiredRootfs && latestRootfsRelease.exact_version === desiredRootfs);
   213	  const openclawBehind = !openclawAlreadyTargetingLatest && isBehindLatestStable(currentOpenclaw, openclawReleases);
   214	  const rootfsBehind = !rootfsAlreadyTargetingLatest && isBehindLatestStable(currentRootfs, rootfsReleases);
   215	  const upgradeAvailable = !upgradeDismissed && (openclawBehind || rootfsBehind);
   216	
   217	  // Loading state
   218	  if (!account || loading) {
   219	    return (

   222	        <div className="h-7 w-48 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.06)] rounded animate-pulse mb-2" />

   231	    return (
   232	      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
   233	        <p className="text-red-400 text-sm">Error: {fetchError}</p>
   234	      </div>
   235	    );
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  if (!machine) {
   239	    return (
   240	      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
   241	        <p className="text-text-secondary text-sm">Machine not found.</p>
   242	      </div>
   243	    );
   244	  }
   245	
   246	  return (

   248	      {/* Back link */}

   250	        to="/dashboard"

    27	function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    28	  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
    29	  if (loading) return <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-screen bg-gray-950" />;
    30	  if (!user) {
    31	    // Redirect to Firebase login page with return URL
    32	    const returnPath = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
    33	    window.location.href = `/login?return=${encodeURIComponent(returnPath)}`;
    34	    return <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-screen bg-gray-950" />;
    35	  }
    36	  return <>{children}</>;
    37	}

     1	import type { User, Account, AccountMember, AccountInvitation, Machine, MachineSize, RegionOption, FileEntry, MachineCredential, CredentialProvider, AccountUsage, MachineUsageDetail, MachineBudgetResponse, ModelEntry, UsageBreakdown, ProviderCatalogEntry, OpikTraceListResponse, OpikTraceDetail, OpikFeedbackScore } from "./types";
     2	import { ApiError } from "./errors";
     3	
     4	// In production VITE_API_URL is set at build time (e.g. "https://api.openclawmachines.com/api").
     5	// In dev the vite proxy forwards /api to localhost:8080.
     6	const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "/api";
     7	
     8	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
     9	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    10	    "Content-Type": "application/json",
    11	    ...(init?.headers as Record<string, string>),
    12	  };
    13	  // ocm_token cookie is sent automatically via credentials: "include"
    14	  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    15	    ...init,
    16	    credentials: "include",
    17	    headers,
    18	  });
    19	  if (!res.ok) {
    20	    // Expired or invalid token — force logout immediately
    21	    if (res.status === 401 && !path.startsWith("/auth/")) {
    22	      window.location.href = "/signed-out";
    23	      throw new ApiError("Session expired", "unauthorized", 401, false);
    24	    }
    25	    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: res.statusText }));
    26	    throw new ApiError(
    27	      body.error || res.statusText,
    28	      body.code || "unknown",
    29	      res.status,
    30	      res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500,
    31	    );
    32	  }
    33	  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
    34	  return res.json();
    35	}

    46	    window.gtag("event", "sign_up", { method: "waitlist", source: source });
    47	  }
    48	  return result;
    49	};

     1	import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
     2	import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
     3	import { ArrowLeft, Globe, Loader2, RefreshCw, X } from "lucide-react";
     4	import { useAuth } from "../lib/auth";
     5	import { getBrowserVM, browserVMLiveUrl } from "../lib/api";
     6	import type { BrowserVM } from "../lib/types";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * Full-screen live view of a paired browser VM. Opens in a new tab via
    10	 * `target="_blank"` from the machine's Browser tab.
    11	 *
    12	 * Layout: a thin top bar (back/status/refresh) over the iframe which
    13	 * fills the rest of the viewport. The iframe points at the backend's
    14	 * /browser-vms/:id/live/ proxy.
    15	 */
    16	export function BrowserVMLivePage() {
    17	  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
    18	  const navigate = useNavigate();
    19	  const { account } = useAuth();
    20	  const accountId = account?.id;
    21	
    22	  const [bvm, setBvm] = useState<BrowserVM | null>(null);
    23	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    24	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    25	  const [iframeKey, setIframeKey] = useState(0);
    26	
    27	  useEffect(() => {
    28	    if (!id || !accountId) return;
    29	    let cancelled = false;
    30	    setLoading(true);
    31	    getBrowserVM(accountId, id)
    32	      .then((data) => {
    33	        if (!cancelled) {
    34	          setBvm(data);
    35	          setError(null);
    36	        }
    37	      })
    38	      .catch((err) => {
    39	        if (!cancelled) {
    40	          setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load browser VM");
    41	        }
    42	      })
    43	      .finally(() => {
    44	        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
    45	      });
    46	    return () => {
    47	      cancelled = true;
    48	    };
    49	  }, [id, accountId]);
    50	
    51	  const handleRefresh = () => setIframeKey((k) => k + 1);
    52	  const handleClose = () => {
    53	    if (window.opener) {
    54	      window.close();
    55	    } else {
    56	      navigate(-1);
    57	    }
    58	  };
    59	
    60	  if (!accountId) {
    61	    return (
    62	      <div className="h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-deep text-text-primary">
    63	        Sign in required
    64	      </div>
    65	    );
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  return (
    69	    <div className="h-screen w-screen flex flex-col bg-black">
    70	      {/* Header bar — kept minimal so the iframe gets almost all the vertical space */}
    71	      <header className="flex items-center justify-between h-10 px-3 bg-card border-b border-border flex-shrink-0">
    72	        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
    73	          <button
    74	            onClick={handleClose}
    75	            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-text-tertiary hover:text-text-primary transition-colors"
    76	            title={window.opener ? "Close tab" : "Back"}
    77	          >
    78	            {window.opener ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />}
    79	          </button>
    80	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
    81	            <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0" />
    82	            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-text-primary truncate">
    83	              {bvm?.slug ?? (loading ? "Loading…" : "Browser VM")}
    84	            </span>
    85	            {bvm && (
    86	              <span
    87	                className={`text-[11px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium flex-shrink-0 ${
    88	                  bvm.status === "running"
    89	                    ? "bg-green-500/10 text-green-400 border border-green-500/20"
    90	                    : "bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/20"
    91	                }`}
    92	              >
    93	                {bvm.status}
    94	              </span>
    95	            )}
    96	            {bvm?.vm_ip && (
    97	              <span className="text-xs text-text-tertiary truncate hidden md:inline">
    98	                CDP {bvm.vm_ip}:{bvm.cdp_port}
    99	              </span>
   100	            )}
   101	          </div>
   102	        </div>
   103	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
   104	          <button
   105	            onClick={handleRefresh}
   106	            disabled={!bvm || bvm.status !== "running"}
   107	            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-text-tertiary hover:text-text-primary disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
   108	            title="Reload live view"
   109	          >
   110	            <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
   111	          </button>
   112	        </div>
   113	      </header>
   114	
   115	      {/* Live view — fills remaining viewport */}
   116	      <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 relative">
   117	        {loading && (
   118	          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-text-tertiary gap-2">
   119	            <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
   120	            Loading browser VM…
   121	          </div>
   122	        )}
   123	        {!loading && error && (
   124	          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400 px-4 text-center">
   125	            {error}
   126	          </div>
   127	        )}
   128	        {!loading && !error && bvm && bvm.status === "running" && (
   129	          <iframe
   130	            key={iframeKey}
   131	            title={`Live browser ${bvm.slug}`}
   132	            src={browserVMLiveUrl(accountId, bvm.id)}
   133	            className="w-full h-full border-0 bg-black"
   134	            allow="clipboard-read; clipboard-write; fullscreen; autoplay"
   135	          />
   136	        )}
   137	        {!loading && !error && bvm && bvm.status !== "running" && (
   138	          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-text-tertiary">
   139	            Live preview is available when the browser VM is running (current: {bvm.status}).
   140	          </div>
   141	        )}
   142	      </main>
   143	    </div>
   144	  );
   145	}

   460	      {activeTab === "backups" && accountId && (
   461	        <BackupsTab machine={machine} accountId={accountId} onRefresh={fetchMachine} />
   462	      )}
   463	
   464	      {/* Delete confirmation dialog */}
   465	      <ConfirmDialog
   466	        open={showDeleteConfirm}
   467	        onOpenChange={setShowDeleteConfirm}
   468	        title="Delete Machine"
   469	        description={`Are you sure you want to delete "${machine.name}"? All workspace data and secrets will be permanently deleted. This action cannot be undone.`}
   470	        confirmLabel="Delete"
   471	        confirmVariant="danger"
   472	        onConfirm={handleDelete}
   473	        confirmText={machine.name}
   474	      />
   475	    </div>
   476	  );
   477	}

    87	func (d directFirecrackerRuntimeOwner) stop(ctx context.Context, _ string, machine *firecracker.Machine, cancel context.CancelFunc, graceTimeout time.Duration) error {
    88		var stopErr error
    89		if machine != nil {
    90			if graceTimeout <= 0 {
    91				graceTimeout = 10 * time.Second
    92			}
    93			shutdownCtx, shutdownCancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, graceTimeout)
    94			defer shutdownCancel()
    95	
    96			if err := machine.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
    97				stopErr = err
    98				_ = machine.StopVMM()
    99			}
   100		}
   101		if cancel != nil {
   102			cancel()
   103		}
   104		return stopErr
   105	}

   144	func (s systemdUnitRuntimeOwner) start(ctx context.Context, vmID string, browser bool, socketPath string, fcCfg firecracker.Config, stdout, stderr io.Writer) (*firecracker.Machine, int, context.CancelFunc, error) {
   145		_ = stdout
   146		_ = stderr
   147		startCtx := ctx
   148		if startCtx == nil {
   149			startCtx = context.Background()
   150		}
   151		if err := systemdAvailable(ctx); err != nil {
   152			return nil, 0, nil, err
   153		}
   154		if firecrackerBinaryPath == "" {
   155			return nil, 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("systemd runtime owner requires firecracker in PATH")
   156		}
   157	
   158		unit := s.ownerRef(vmID, browser)
   159		vmCtx, vmCancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
   160		machine, err := firecracker.NewMachine(vmCtx, fcCfg)
   161		if err != nil {
   162			vmCancel()
   163			return nil, 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("create firecracker machine: %w", err)
   164		}
   165		machine.Handlers.FcInit = machine.Handlers.FcInit.Swap(firecracker.Handler{
   166			Name: firecracker.StartVMMHandlerName,
   167			Fn: func(handlerCtx context.Context, m *firecracker.Machine) error {
   168				return startFirecrackerUnit(handlerCtx, m, unit, socketPath, vmID)
   169			},
   170		})
   171		// Keep the machine's lifetime detached from the request after boot, but
   172		// make the boot handshake itself respect the caller's deadline.
   173		if err := machine.Start(startCtx); err != nil {
   174			vmCancel()
   175			return nil, 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("start firecracker via systemd-unit: %w", err)
   176		}
   177	
   178		state, err := systemdUnitStateReader(unit)
   179		if err != nil {
   180			vmCancel()
   181			_ = systemdUnitStopper(unit)
   182			return nil, 0, nil, err
   183		}
   184		if state.MainPID <= 0 {
   185			vmCancel()
   186			_ = systemdUnitStopper(unit)
   187			return nil, 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid systemd MainPID for %s: %d", unit, state.MainPID)
   188		}
   189	
   190		return machine, state.MainPID, vmCancel, nil
   191	}
   192	
   193	func (s systemdUnitRuntimeOwner) stop(ctx context.Context, ownerRef string, machine *firecracker.Machine, cancel context.CancelFunc, graceTimeout time.Duration) error {
   194		var stopErr error
   195		if ownerRef == "" {
   196			stopErr = fmt.Errorf("missing systemd ownerRef for VM stop")
   197		} else {
   198			shutdownCtx := ctx
   199			var shutdownCancel context.CancelFunc
   200			if graceTimeout > 0 {
   201				shutdownCtx, shutdownCancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, graceTimeout)
   202				defer shutdownCancel()
   203			}
   204			if err := systemdUnitStopperContext(shutdownCtx, ownerRef); err != nil {
   205				stopErr = err
   206			}
   207		}
   208		if cancel != nil {
   209			cancel()
   210		}
   211		return stopErr
   212	}

   216	var systemdDBusConnector = func(ctx context.Context) (*dbus.Conn, error) {
   217		return dbus.NewWithContext(ctx)

   220	var systemdDBusCloser = func(conn *dbus.Conn) error {
   221		conn.Close()
   222		return nil
   223	}
   224	
   225	var systemdStartTransientUnit = func(ctx context.Context, conn *dbus.Conn, name string, mode string, properties []dbus.Property, ch chan<- string) (int, error) {
   226		return conn.StartTransientUnitContext(ctx, name, mode, properties, ch)
   227	}
   228	
   229	var firecrackerSocketProbe = func(socketPath string) error {
   230		client := firecracker.NewClient(socketPath, logrus.NewEntry(logrus.New()), false)
   231		_, err := client.GetMachineConfiguration()
   232		return err
   233	}
   234	
   235	func resolveFirecrackerBinaryPath() string {
   236		path, err := exec.LookPath("firecracker")
   237		if err != nil {
   238			return ""
   239		}
   240		return path
   241	}
   242	
   243	func systemdAvailable(ctx context.Context) error {
   244		if _, err := os.Stat("/run/systemd/system"); err != nil {
   245			return fmt.Errorf("systemd runtime owner requires a running systemd host: %w", err)
   246		}
   247		conn, err := systemdDBusConnector(ctx)
   248		if err != nil {
   249			return fmt.Errorf("connect to systemd dbus: %w", err)
   250		}
   251		if conn != nil {
   252			defer func() { _ = systemdDBusCloser(conn) }()
   253		}
   254		return nil
   255	}
   256	
   257	func startFirecrackerUnit(ctx context.Context, machine *firecracker.Machine, unit, socketPath, vmID string) error {
   258		conn, err := systemdDBusConnector(ctx)
   259		if err != nil {
   260			return fmt.Errorf("connect to systemd dbus: %w", err)
   261		}
   262		if conn != nil {
   263			defer func() { _ = systemdDBusCloser(conn) }()
   264		}
   265	
   266		// Register the unit-stop cleanup BEFORE StartTransientUnit. The
   267		// firecracker-go-sdk's machine.Start defers doCleanup() on any handler
   268		// error, so any failure after this point — including a ctx cancel between
   269		// a successful StartTransientUnit and the end of waitForSystemdUnitActive
   270		// — will now tear the unit back down. Registering after the wait was a
   271		// stranding bug: a mid-boot cancel left an active ocm-vm-*.service + live
   272		// Firecracker with no in-memory record and no cleanup hook.
   273		//
   274		// systemdUnitStopper treats a missing unit as success, so running this
   275		// cleanup before the unit actually exists (e.g. StartTransientUnit itself
   276		// fails) is a no-op.
   277		appendMachineCleanupFunc(machine, func() error {
   278			stopErr := systemdUnitStopper(unit)
   279			removeErr := os.Remove(socketPath)
   280			if os.IsNotExist(removeErr) {
   281				removeErr = nil
   282			}
   283			if stopErr != nil {
   284				return stopErr
   285			}
   286			return removeErr
   287		})
   288	
   289		resultCh := make(chan string, 1)
   290		if _, err := systemdStartTransientUnit(ctx, conn, unit, "replace", systemdTransientUnitProperties(socketPath, vmID), resultCh); err != nil {
   291			return fmt.Errorf("start transient unit %s: %w", unit, err)
   292		}
   293	
   294		select {
   295		case result := <-resultCh:
   296			if result != "done" && result != "skipped" {
   297				return fmt.Errorf("start transient unit %s: job result %q", unit, result)
   298			}
   299		case <-ctx.Done():
   300			return fmt.Errorf("start transient unit %s: %w", unit, ctx.Err())
   301		}
   302	
   303		if _, err := waitForSystemdUnitActive(ctx, unit); err != nil {
   304			return err
   305		}
   306	
   307		if err := waitForFirecrackerSocket(ctx, socketPath); err != nil {
   308			return fmt.Errorf("firecracker socket %s not ready: %w", socketPath, err)
   309		}
   310		return nil
   311	}
   312	
   313	func systemdTransientUnitProperties(socketPath, vmID string) []dbus.Property {
   314		return []dbus.Property{
   315			dbus.PropExecStart([]string{firecrackerBinaryPath, "--api-sock", socketPath, "--id", vmID, "--no-seccomp"}, false),
   316			dbus.PropType("exec"),
   317			dbus.PropDescription("OCM Firecracker VM " + vmID),
   318			{Name: "Restart", Value: godbus.MakeVariant("no")},
   319			{Name: "KillMode", Value: godbus.MakeVariant("process")},
   320			{Name: "CollectMode", Value: godbus.MakeVariant("inactive-or-failed")},
   321		}
   322	}
   323	
   324	// waitForSystemdUnitActiveTransientErrorBudget is the number of consecutive
   325	// systemdUnitStateReader failures tolerated before giving up. A brief
   326	// dbus/systemd hiccup (the socket is momentarily unavailable, or a
   327	// GetUnitProperties call races unit lookup) must not be fatal — otherwise
   328	// VM startup is flaky under load. The budget is reset to zero on any
   329	// successful read, so sustained failure still surfaces.
   330	const waitForSystemdUnitActiveTransientErrorBudget = 5
   331	
   332	func waitForSystemdUnitActive(ctx context.Context, unit string) (int, error) {
   333		ticker := time.NewTicker(20 * time.Millisecond)
   334		defer ticker.Stop()
   335	
   336		transientErrors := 0
   337		var lastErr error
   338	
   339		for {
   340			state, err := systemdUnitStateReader(unit)
   341			if err != nil {
   342				// Terminal errors (unit genuinely missing) map to LoadState
   343				// not-found elsewhere; this branch only fires on transient
   344				// read failures. Give dbus a few retries before giving up.
   345				transientErrors++
   346				lastErr = err
   347				if transientErrors > waitForSystemdUnitActiveTransientErrorBudget {
   348					return 0, fmt.Errorf("read systemd unit state for %s after %d retries: %w", unit, transientErrors-1, err)
   349				}
   350				slog.Warn("waitForSystemdUnitActive.transient_read_error",
   351					"unit", unit,
   352					"retry", transientErrors,
   353					"budget", waitForSystemdUnitActiveTransientErrorBudget,
   354					"error", err)
   355	
   356				select {
   357				case <-ctx.Done():
   358					return 0, fmt.Errorf("wait for systemd unit %s active: %w (last transient error: %v)", unit, ctx.Err(), lastErr)
   359				case <-ticker.C:
   360				}
   361				continue
   362			}
   363			// Successful read — reset the transient-error budget so we can
   364			// tolerate another burst later in the boot if one arrives.
   365			transientErrors = 0
   366	
   367			if state.ActiveState == "active" && state.MainPID > 0 {
   368				return state.MainPID, nil
   369			}
   370			// Fast-fail on terminal states so callers see the real failure
   371			// immediately instead of burning the full boot deadline waiting
   372			// for a dead unit to become active. Before this, a crashed
   373			// ExecStart would block the entire startup timeout.
   374			//
   375			// "failed"    — ExecStart crashed / returned non-zero.
   376			// "not-found" — the transient unit was GC'd before we could
   377			//               confirm activation (e.g. CollectMode=inactive-or-failed
   378			//               fired early).
   379			if state.ActiveState == "failed" {
   380				return 0, fmt.Errorf("systemd unit %s entered failed state (sub=%q)", unit, state.SubState)
   381			}
   382			if state.LoadState == "not-found" {
   383				return 0, fmt.Errorf("systemd unit %s vanished before becoming active", unit)
   384			}
   385	
   386			select {
   387			case <-ctx.Done():
   388				return 0, fmt.Errorf("wait for systemd unit %s active: %w", unit, ctx.Err())
   389			case <-ticker.C:
   390			}
   391		}
   392	}
   393	
   394	func waitForFirecrackerSocket(ctx context.Context, socketPath string) error {
   395		ticker := time.NewTicker(10 * time.Millisecond)
   396		defer ticker.Stop()
   397	
   398		for {
   399			if _, err := os.Stat(socketPath); err == nil {
   400				if err := firecrackerSocketProbe(socketPath); err == nil {
   401					return nil
   402				}
   403			}
   404	
   405			select {
   406			case <-ctx.Done():
   407				return ctx.Err()
   408			case <-ticker.C:
   409			}
   410		}
   411	}
   412	
   413	func appendMachineCleanupFunc(machine *firecracker.Machine, fn func() error) {
   414		if machine == nil || fn == nil {
   415			return
   416		}
   417		field := reflect.ValueOf(machine).Elem().FieldByName("cleanupFuncs")
   418		cleanupFuncs := (*[]func() error)(unsafe.Pointer(field.UnsafeAddr()))
   419		*cleanupFuncs = append(*cleanupFuncs, fn)
   420	}

   153	
   154	// HandleLogs is the SSE handler for log streaming.
   155	// Query param: machine_id (required)
   156	func (s *Server) HandleLogs(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   157		mi, err := s.getMachineInfo(r)
   158		if err != nil {
   159			// Fallback to query param if URL path doesn't have machineID (for backward compat or direct calls)
   160			machineID := r.URL.Query().Get("machine_id")
   161			if machineID == "" {
   162				http.Error(w, "machine_id required", http.StatusBadRequest)
   163				return
   164			}
   165			vm, err := s.orchestrator.Get(r.Context(), machineID)
   166			if err != nil {
   167				http.Error(w, "VM not found", http.StatusNotFound)
   168				return
   169			}
   170			mi = &machineInfo{MachineID: machineID, ProxyToken: vm.ProxyToken}
   171		}
   172	
   173		if !validateProxyToken(w, r, mi.ProxyToken) {
   174			return
   175		}
   176	
   177		flusher, ok := w.(http.Flusher)
   178		if !ok {
   179			http.Error(w, "streaming not supported", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   180			return
   181		}
   182	
   183		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
   184		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
   185		w.Header().Set("Connection", "keep-alive")
   186	
   187		ch := s.logMgr.Subscribe(mi.MachineID)
   188		defer s.logMgr.Unsubscribe(mi.MachineID, ch)
   189	
   190		// SSE keepalive: send comment lines to prevent proxy idle timeouts (~100s on Cloudflare).
   191		// SSE spec says lines starting with ":" are comments, ignored by EventSource clients.
   192		keepalive := time.NewTicker(30 * time.Second)
   193		defer keepalive.Stop()
   194	
   195		for {
   196			select {
   197			case <-r.Context().Done():
   198				return
   199			case <-keepalive.C:
   200				_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, ": keepalive\n\n")
   201				flusher.Flush()
   202			case entry, ok := <-ch:
   203				if !ok {
   204					return
   205				}
   206				if entry.Replay {
   207					_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "event: replay\ndata: %s\n\n", entry.Message)
   208				} else {
   209					_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "data: %s\n\n", entry.Message)
   210				}
   211				flusher.Flush()
   212			}
   213		}
   214	}

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba frontend/src/components/ProvisioningProgress.tsx | sed -n '55,115p'" in /home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines
 succeeded in 0ms:
    55	    const base = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "/api";
    56	    const url = `${base}/accounts/${accountId}/machines/${machineId}/progress`;
    57	    const maxRetries = 40; // ~5 minutes of retrying (40 * 8s)
    58	    let retryCount = 0;
    59	    let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    60	    let cancelled = false;
    61	
    62	    function connect() {
    63	      if (cancelled) return;
    64	      const es = new EventSource(url, { withCredentials: true });
    65	      eventSourceRef.current = es;
    66	
    67	      es.onmessage = (event) => {
    68	        try {
    69	          const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
    70	          receivedAnyStep.current = true;
    71	          retryCount = 0; // reset retries on success
    72	          if (data.step === "error") {
    73	            setErrorMessage(data.message || "Provisioning failed");
    74	            es.close();
    75	            return;
    76	          }
    77	          if (!stepStartTimes.current[data.step]) {
    78	            stepStartTimes.current[data.step] = Date.now();
    79	          }
    80	          setCurrentStep(data.step);
    81	          if (data.step === "machine_ready") {
    82	            setCompleted(true);
    83	            es.close();
    84	            onCompleteRef.current?.();
    85	          }
    86	        } catch (e) {
    87	          console.error("[ProvisioningProgress] Failed to parse SSE message:", event.data, e);
    88	        }
    89	      };
    90	
    91	      es.onerror = () => {
    92	        es.close();
    93	        if (cancelled) return;
    94	        // If we've received steps before, this is a mid-stream disconnect — always retry
    95	        // If no steps yet, the host VM is likely still booting — retry until max
    96	        if (retryCount < maxRetries) {
    97	          retryCount++;
    98	          setConnectAttempt(retryCount);
    99	          retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, 8000);
   100	        } else {
   101	          setErrorMessage("Failed to connect to provisioning service");
   102	        }
   103	      };
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    connect();
   107	
   108	    return () => {
   109	      cancelled = true;
   110	      if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
   111	      eventSourceRef.current?.close();
   112	    };
   113	  }, [accountId, machineId]);
   114	
   115	  const currentOrder = currentStep

   105	func assignScanValue(t *testing.T, dest any, value any) {
   106		t.Helper()
   107	
   108		switch d := dest.(type) {
   109		case *string:
   110			*d = value.(string)
   111		case **string:
   112			if value == nil {
   113				*d = nil
   114				return
   115			}
   116			v := value.(string)
   117			*d = &v
   118		case *int:
   119			*d = value.(int)
   120		case **int:
   121			if value == nil {
   122				*d = nil
   123				return
   124			}
   125			v := value.(int)
   126			*d = &v
   127		case *int64:
   128			*d = value.(int64)
   129		case **int64:
   130			if value == nil {
   131				*d = nil
   132				return
   133			}
   134			v := value.(int64)
   135			*d = &v
   136		case *bool:
   137			*d = value.(bool)
   138		case *time.Time:
   139			*d = value.(time.Time)
   140		case **time.Time:
   141			if value == nil {
   142				*d = nil
   143				return
   144			}
   145			v := value.(time.Time)
   146			*d = &v
   147		case *[]byte:
   148			if value == nil {
   149				*d = nil
   150				return
   151			}
   152			v := value.([]byte)
   153			*d = append([]byte(nil), v...)
   154		default:
   155			t.Fatalf("unsupported scan destination type %T", dest)
   156		}
   157	}

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba docs/CurrentFeature.md | sed -n '1,140p'" in /home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Current Feature: instrumentation
     2	
     3	## Goal
     4	
     5	End users see live CPU and memory usage for every running VM (OpenClaw + Browser) on their machine detail page. 1-second resolution, 24-hour history, with older data downsampled to 1-minute averages and kept 7 days.
     6	
     7	**Detailed design:** [docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md](plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md)
     8	
     9	## User-Facing Behavior
    10	
    11	New "Usage" tab on `/machines/:id` (and equivalent for browser VMs) with **two sub-views**:
    12	
    13	**Live view** (default)
    14	- Sliding 5-minute window, refreshes every 1s.
    15	- Two line charts: CPU % and Memory MB.
    16	- Big-number current values above the charts.
    17	- Empty state: "No data yet — VM is starting" while first sample lands.
    18	
    19	**History view**
    20	- Date/time range picker (default: last 24 h).
    21	- Resolution auto: 1s for ranges ≤ 1h, 1m for longer.
    22	- Static charts; pan + zoom.
    23	- Available even after VM stops, for the retention window (1s: 24h, 1m: 7d).
    24	
    25	## Architecture
    26	
    27	```
    28	[Host: agent] --1Hz sample--> [in-mem 10s buffer] --POST every 10s--> [backend API]
    29	                                                                          |
    30	                                                                          v
    31	                                                              [Postgres: vm_metrics_1s]
    32	                                                                          |
    33	                                                  hourly downsample cron  v
    34	                                                              [Postgres: vm_metrics_1m]
    35	                                                                          ^
    36	[Frontend] --GET /machines/:id/metrics?range=5m&res=1s--------------------+
    37	```
    38	
    39	### Source: cgroup v2 on the host
    40	
    41	Each VM runs in its own systemd unit (`ocm-vm-<id>.service` or `ocm-browser-vm-<id>.service`), so cgroup v2 already isolates per-VM resources at:
    42	
    43	- `/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/<unit>/cpu.stat` — `usage_usec` field, cumulative
    44	- `/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/<unit>/memory.current` — bytes, instantaneous
    45	
    46	CPU % computed as `delta_usage_usec / delta_wall_usec * 100` per sample (can exceed 100% on multi-core, which is correct).
    47	
    48	### Storage: Neon Postgres
    49	
    50	Two tables, partitioned by day for cheap retention:
    51	
    52	```sql
    53	CREATE TABLE vm_metrics_1s (
    54	  vm_id      uuid       NOT NULL,
    55	  ts         timestamptz NOT NULL,
    56	  cpu_pct    real        NOT NULL,
    57	  mem_bytes  bigint      NOT NULL,
    58	  PRIMARY KEY (vm_id, ts)
    59	) PARTITION BY RANGE (ts);
    60	
    61	CREATE TABLE vm_metrics_1m (LIKE vm_metrics_1s INCLUDING ALL) PARTITION BY RANGE (ts);
    62	```
    63	
    64	Daily partitions; cleanup cron drops 1s partitions older than 24h, 1m partitions older than 7d.
    65	
    66	### Endpoints
    67	
    68	- `POST /api/v1/internal/vm-metrics` — agent batch upload (auth via existing agent token)
    69	- `GET  /api/v1/machines/:id/metrics?from=…&to=…` — user-facing query, resolution chosen by range (1s if ≤1h, 1m otherwise). Auth: machine ownership check.
    70	- `GET  /api/v1/machines/:id/metrics/live` — Server-Sent Events stream of 1s points for the live view.
    71	- Same two endpoints under `/api/v1/browser-vms/:id/metrics[…]` for browser VMs.
    72	
    73	### Cron jobs (backend)
    74	
    75	- **Downsample**: every 1 min, take 1s rows older than 1h, average into 1m bucket, insert into `vm_metrics_1m`. Idempotent (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`).
    76	- **Cleanup**: every 1 hour, drop daily partitions of `vm_metrics_1s` older than 24h.
    77	
    78	### Agent changes
    79	
    80	New package `backend/internal/vmmetrics/sampler` that:
    81	1. Discovers cgroups by scanning `/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/` for `ocm-vm-*.service` + `ocm-browser-vm-*.service`.
    82	2. Samples each at 1Hz on a single goroutine.
    83	3. Buffers in a ring (last 30s) and posts batches every 10s.
    84	4. On post failure, retries with exponential backoff; drops oldest if buffer fills.
    85	
    86	## Test Plan (write before code)
    87	
    88	### Backend unit tests
    89	
    90	- [ ] `cgroup.Sample(unit)` — fixture cgroup dir with `cpu.stat`/`memory.current` parses correctly.
    91	- [ ] `cgroup.Sample(unit)` — missing files return error (not panic).
    92	- [ ] `cgroup.CPUPercent(prev, curr)` — delta over wall time = correct %.
    93	- [ ] `cgroup.DiscoverVMs(root)` — returns ocm-vm-* and ocm-browser-vm-* units, ignores others.
    94	- [ ] `metrics.Store.Insert(batch)` — round-trips a batch.
    95	- [ ] `metrics.Store.Query(vmID, range, res)` — returns points within range; picks 1s vs 1m by range.
    96	- [ ] `metrics.Downsample(from, to)` — 60 1s points → 1 1m point with correct avg.
    97	- [ ] `metrics.DropOldPartitions()` — drops partitions older than retention.
    98	
    99	### Backend integration tests
   100	
   101	- [ ] Agent POST → DB → user GET round-trip on a real Postgres.
   102	- [ ] User cannot read another user's machine metrics (403).
   103	- [ ] Range selection picks correct table (5m → 1s, 24h → 1m).
   104	- [ ] Downsample cron is idempotent — running twice doesn't double-count.
   105	
   106	### Frontend tests
   107	
   108	- [ ] `<UsageTab>` renders charts when given mock data.
   109	- [ ] Live polling: setInterval triggers refetch every 1s while tab is visible.
   110	- [ ] Range picker switches resolution.
   111	
   112	## Build sequence
   113	
   114	1. Migration + schema + Store unit tests
   115	2. cgroup sampler + sampler unit tests
   116	3. Agent integration: POST endpoint + agent loop
   117	4. Downsample + cleanup crons
   118	5. User GET endpoint + ownership check
   119	6. Frontend Usage tab + live polling
   120	7. End-to-end smoke on a dev host
   121	
   122	## Open questions / risks
   123	
   124	- **Cardinality**: 100 concurrent VMs × 86400s/day = 8.6M 1s rows/day across all users. Daily partitions keep query/scan bounded.
   125	- **Browser VMs**: same cgroup story — confirmed all VMs use per-unit systemd.
   126	- **Agent CPU overhead**: 1Hz × ~10 VMs/host = trivial. Will measure during e2e.
   127	- **Older browser VMs (legacy direct-owner)**: if any still exist, they won't have a per-unit cgroup. Sampler skips silently rather than failing.

codex
I’ve found several concrete mismatches now: the API in the repo is account-scoped `/api/accounts/{accountId}/...`, agent heartbeat uses integer host IDs, and the existing agent auth helper has a fleet-token bypass that would be dangerous for metrics unless the new endpoint follows heartbeat auth exactly. I’m doing one more pass for data-retention and SSE edge cases before summarizing.

   362	func validateRootfsVersion(version string) error {
   363		version = strings.TrimSpace(version)
   364		switch {
   365		case version == "":
   366			return fmt.Errorf("rootfs version is required")
   367		case strings.Contains(version, ".."):
   368			return fmt.Errorf("invalid rootfs version %q", version)
   369		case strings.ContainsAny(version, `/\`):
   370			return fmt.Errorf("invalid rootfs version %q", version)
   371		default:
   372			return nil
   373		}
   374	}

   394	// checkOpenClawRootfsPairCompat enforces the runtime-compat guard for an
   395	// openclaw+rootfs version pair: if the openclaw release carries a
   396	// min_rootfs_version, rootfsVersion must be at least that new. Fails open
   397	// when the openclaw release or either rootfs version is not in
   398	// artifact_releases (dev builds, stale catalog) so the guard doesn't
   399	// over-reject. Returns *store.IncompatibleRootfsError when the pair is unsafe.
   400	func (s *Server) checkOpenClawRootfsPairCompat(ctx context.Context, openclawVersion, rootfsVersion string) error {
   401		if rootfsVersion == "" {
   402			return nil
   403		}
   404		release, err := s.store.GetArtifactRelease(ctx, "openclaw", openclawVersion)
   405		if err != nil || release == nil || release.MinRootfsVersion == nil {
   406			return nil
   407		}
   408		minVer := *release.MinRootfsVersion
   409		if rootfsVersion == minVer {
   410			return nil
   411		}
   412		minRow, err := s.store.GetArtifactRelease(ctx, "rootfs", minVer)
   413		if err != nil || minRow == nil {
   414			return nil
   415		}
   416		curRow, err := s.store.GetArtifactRelease(ctx, "rootfs", rootfsVersion)
   417		if err != nil || curRow == nil {
   418			return nil
   419		}
   420		return store.CheckOpenClawRootfsCompat(release, rootfsVersion, minRow.CreatedAt, curRow.CreatedAt)
   421	}

  1736	func (s *Server) handleStartMachine(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
  1737		accountID := accountIDFromContext(r.Context())
  1738		claims := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())
  1739		id := chi.URLParam(r, "id")
  1740	
  1741		machine, err := s.store.GetMachine(r.Context(), id)
  1742		if err != nil {
  1743			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "machine not found")
  1744			return
  1745		}
  1746	
  1747		if machine.AccountID != accountID {
  1748			writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, "machine does not belong to this account")
  1749			return
  1750		}
  1751	
  1752		if machine.Status == "running" {
  1753			writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, "machine already running")
  1754			return
  1755		}
  1756	
  1757		host, vmIP, err := s.machines.Start(r.Context(), accountID, machine)
  1758		if err != nil {
  1759			errMsg := err.Error()
  1760			s.activity.Log(r.Context(), events.LogParams{
  1761				Category:  "machine",
  1762				Action:    "machine.start_failed",
  1763				Status:    "failure",
  1764				ActorType: "user",
  1765				ActorID:   &claims.UserID,
  1766				AccountID: &accountID,
  1767				MachineID: &machine.ID,
  1768				Summary:   fmt.Sprintf("Failed to start '%s': %s", machine.Name, err),
  1769				ErrorMsg:  &errMsg,
  1770			})
  1771			writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, err.Error())
  1772			return
  1773		}
  1774	
  1775		hostID := host.ID
  1776		s.activity.Log(r.Context(), events.LogParams{
  1777			Category:  "machine",
  1778			Action:    "machine.started",
  1779			Status:    "success",
  1780			ActorType: "user",
  1781			ActorID:   &claims.UserID,
  1782			AccountID: &accountID,
  1783			MachineID: &machine.ID,
  1784			HostID:    &hostID,
  1785			Summary:   fmt.Sprintf("Started '%s' on %s", machine.Name, host.VMName),
  1786		})
  1787	
  1788		// "starting" for restart (has host assignment), "provisioning" for first boot
  1789		status := "provisioning"
  1790		if machine.HostID != nil {
  1791			status = "starting"
  1792		}
  1793	
  1794		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]interface{}{
  1795			"status":  status,
  1796			"host_id": host.ID,
  1797			"vm_ip":   vmIP,
  1798		})
  1799	}

   741	type contextKey string

   743	const (
   744		accountMemberKey contextKey = "accountMember"
   745		accountIDKey     contextKey = "accountID"
   746	)
   747	
   748	func (s *Server) AccountMiddleware(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
   749		return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   750			accountID, err := parseIntParam(r, "accountId")
   751			if err != nil {
   752				writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid account id")
   753				return
   754			}
   755	
   756			claims := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())
   757			if claims == nil {
   758				writeError(w, http.StatusUnauthorized, "unauthorized")
   759				return
   760			}
   761	
   762			member, err := s.store.GetAccountMember(r.Context(), accountID, claims.UserID)
   763			if err != nil {
   764				writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, "access denied to this account")
   765				return
   766			}
   767	
   768			ctx := context.WithValue(r.Context(), accountMemberKey, member)
   769			ctx = context.WithValue(ctx, accountIDKey, accountID)
   770			next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(ctx))
   771		})
   772	}
   773	
   774	// requireSuperuser blocks requests from non-superuser accounts.
   775	func (s *Server) requireSuperuser(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
   776		return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   777			if !isSuperuser(r.Context()) {
   778				writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, "admin access required")
   779				return
   780			}
   781			next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
   782		})
   783	}
   784	
   785	// isSuperuser reports whether the current request is authenticated as the
   786	// superuser. Used by handlers that need to gate individual fields (e.g.
   787	// tenant-supplied host_id/region placement pinning) rather than whole
   788	// routes.
   789	func isSuperuser(ctx context.Context) bool {
   790		claims := auth.UserFromContext(ctx)
   791		return claims != nil && claims.Email == "mathewma@gmail.com"
   792	}
   793	
   794	// accountIDFromContext returns the account ID set by AccountMiddleware.
   795	func accountIDFromContext(ctx context.Context) int {
   796		id, _ := ctx.Value(accountIDKey).(int)
   797		return id
   798	}
   799	
   800	// userIDFromContext returns a pointer to the authenticated user's ID, or nil if not available.
   801	func userIDFromContext(ctx context.Context) *int {
   802		claims := auth.UserFromContext(ctx)
   803		if claims == nil || claims.UserID == 0 {
   804			return nil
   805		}
   806		return &claims.UserID
   807	}

   810	func accountMemberFromContext(ctx context.Context) *store.AccountMember {
   811		m, _ := ctx.Value(accountMemberKey).(*store.AccountMember)
   812		return m
   813	}

   107	func (s *Server) handleDeleteBrowserVM(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   108		accountID := accountIDFromContext(r.Context())
   109		bvmID := chi.URLParam(r, "browserVmId")
   110	
   111		// cleanupCtx inherits request values (auth, tracing) but not cancellation.
   112		// Any persistence step that mutates durable state MUST use this context
   113		// so a client disconnect mid-delete cannot strand the DB record with the
   114		// agent-side VM already destroyed. The actual outbound calls (placement,
   115		// agent) stay on r.Context() so clients can still cancel the operation.
   116		cleanupCtx := context.WithoutCancel(r.Context())
   117	
   118		bvm, err := s.store.GetBrowserVM(r.Context(), bvmID)
   119		if err != nil {
   120			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "browser VM not found")
   130		pairedMachine, err := s.store.GetMachineByBrowserVMID(r.Context(), bvmID)
   131		if err == nil && pairedMachine != nil {
   132			s.cleanupBrowserPairing(cleanupCtx, pairedMachine, bvm)
   133			slog.Info("browser_vm.delete.auto_unpaired", "browser_vm_id", bvmID, "machine_id", pairedMachine.ID)
   134		}
   135	
   136		// Stop if running: call agent to destroy. If the agent call fails (e.g.
   137		// host unreachable), keep the DB record so the user can retry — deleting
   138		// it would orphan the Firecracker process on the host with no way to
   139		// track it from the control plane. Status is set to "error" so it's
   140		// visible in the UI.
   141		if (bvm.Status == "running" || bvm.Status == "provisioning") && bvm.HostID != nil {
   142			host, hostErr := s.store.GetHost(r.Context(), *bvm.HostID)
   143			if hostErr != nil || host == nil {
   144				slog.Error("browser_vm.delete.host_lookup_failed", "browser_vm_id", bvmID, "error", hostErr)
   145				errMsg := "host lookup failed — retry delete later"
   146				_ = s.store.UpdateBrowserVMStatus(cleanupCtx, bvmID, "error", &errMsg)
   147				writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "host unavailable — browser VM record kept for retry")
   148				return
   149			}
   150			if s.agentClient != nil {
   151				if err := s.agentClient.DestroyBrowserVM(r.Context(), host, bvmID); err != nil {
   152					slog.Error("browser_vm.delete.destroy_failed", "browser_vm_id", bvmID, "error", err)
   153					errMsg := "agent destroy failed: " + err.Error()
   154					_ = s.store.UpdateBrowserVMStatus(cleanupCtx, bvmID, "error", &errMsg)
   155					writeError(w, http.StatusBadGateway,
   156						"agent destroy failed — browser VM record kept for retry: "+err.Error())
   157					return
   158				}
   159			}
   160			_ = s.store.ReleaseBrowserVMPlacement(cleanupCtx, bvmID)
   161			_ = s.store.UnassignBrowserVMFromHost(cleanupCtx, bvmID)
   162		}
   163	
   164		if err := s.store.DeleteBrowserVM(cleanupCtx, bvmID); err != nil {
   165			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "failed to delete browser VM")
   166			return
   167		}
   168	
   169		slog.Info("browser_vm.deleted", "id", bvmID, "account_id", accountID)
   170		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   171	}
   172	
   173	// callerOccupiesHost reports whether the account currently runs — or is homed
   174	// on — at least one machine on the given host. Used to gate tenant-supplied
   175	// host_id placement: if the caller already knows the host exists via a
   176	// machine they own there, pinning a browser VM to the same host leaks no new
   177	// fleet information.
   178	func (s *Server) callerOccupiesHost(ctx context.Context, accountID, hostID int) (bool, error) {
   179		machines, err := s.store.ListMachinesByAccount(ctx, accountID)
   180		if err != nil {
   181			return false, err
   182		}
   183		for _, m := range machines {
   184			if m.HostID != nil && *m.HostID == hostID {
   185				return true, nil
   186			}
   187			if m.HomeHostID != nil && *m.HomeHostID == hostID {
   188				return true, nil
   189			}
   190		}
   191		return false, nil
   192	}

frontend/src/pages/machine-tabs/UsageTab.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { DollarSign, Zap, TrendingUp, BarChart3 } from "lucide-react";
import type { Machine, UsageBreakdown, UsageBucketEntry } from "../../lib/types";
import { getMachineUsageBreakdown } from "../../lib/api";

interface UsageTabProps {
  machine: Machine;
  accountId: number;
}

function formatMicrocents(mc: number): string {
  const dollars = mc / 1_000_000;
  return `$${dollars.toFixed(4)}`;
}

function formatTokens(n: number): string {
  if (n >= 1_000_000) return `${(n / 1_000_000).toFixed(2)}M`;
  if (n >= 1000) return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}K`;
  return String(n);
}

function formatHour(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
}

function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" });
}

type Period = "hour" | "day";

  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<Period>("hour");
  const [breakdown, setBreakdown] = useState<UsageBreakdown | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setLoading(true);

      .then((b) => setBreakdown(b ?? null))

import type { Machine, MachineCredential, ProviderCatalogEntry } from "../../lib/types";
import {
  listProviderCatalog,
  listMachineCredentials,
  putMachineCredential,
  deleteMachineCredential,
  pushMachineConfig,
} from "../../lib/api";
import { useToast } from "../../components/Toast";

interface WebSearchTabProps {

}

codex
**CRITICAL**
- Deleted-VM history is not authorizable as designed. The doc says metrics outlive VM rows and deleted-VM history should still return, but ownership is checked from the live VM row. After hard delete, `machines` is gone, and metrics have no `account_id`, so the backend can either leak data or return 404. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:141), [deleted-history test](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:241), and hard delete in [postgres.go](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/backend/internal/store/postgres.go:604). Persist `account_id` on metrics at ingest, or create a VM tombstone/ownership table.
- Agent auth is underspecified and can become a write-any-VM hole if implemented with the existing helper. `authenticateAgentToken` accepts the fleet token and returns `hostID=0`; `validateMachinePlacement` then skips placement checks. The metrics endpoint must follow heartbeat auth: require integer `host_id`, authenticate token against that claimed host, and never let fleet-token auth bypass placement. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:111), [agent_auth.go](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/backend/internal/api/agent_auth.go:35), and heartbeat’s host-bound path in [agent_heartbeat.go](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/backend/internal/api/agent_heartbeat.go:140). Also the sample `host_id: "h-east-3"` conflicts with existing integer host IDs.

**IMPORTANT**
- The schema contradicts the sampler contract: first sample has omitted CPU, but `cpu_pct REAL NOT NULL` cannot store it. Either do not insert first samples or make `cpu_pct` nullable. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:89) and [schema](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:124).
- Downsampling is not correct for late batches. The doc says `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` and “worst-case error is one data point”, but the agent batches every 10s and backs off up to 60s. A partial minute can be frozen forever, losing 10-60 samples. Use a watermark/finalization delay, store `sample_count`, and make late reruns update the bucket deterministically. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:152) and [late-arrival claim](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:155).
- Daily partitions do not give exact 24h retention. Dropping whole daily partitions means `vm_metrics_1s` retains between 24h and almost 48h depending on UTC time. Either document that retention is daily-granular or use smaller partitions/row deletes. See [partition plan](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:143) and [drop policy](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:153).
- Maintenance jobs need singleton protection. Cloud Run/API mode already starts background loops per instance, so `EnsurePartitions`, `Downsample1s1m`, and `DropOldPartitions` need advisory locks or a real single scheduler. “Existing fleet scheduler” is not a concrete repo convention here. See API background loops in [cmd/server/main.go](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/backend/cmd/server/main.go:334).
- The live SSE design scales with browsers, not VMs. `WHERE vm_id=$1 AND ts>$2` every second per open tab is not “cheap” just because indexed; 100 live tabs is 100 DB polls/sec plus long-lived Cloud Run handlers. Also agent batches every 10s, so “refreshing every second” will be bursty and up to 10s stale. See [live promise](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:14), [batching](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:33), and [polling claim](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:198).
- Routes do not match the codebase. The repo uses `/api`, not `/api/v1`, and user APIs are account-scoped under `/api/accounts/{accountId}`. Existing frontend API helpers also use `BASE="/api"`. Put metrics under `/api/accounts/{accountId}/machines/{id}/metrics` and `/browser-vms/{browserVmId}/metrics`. See [design route](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:164), [router](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/backend/internal/api/server.go:362), and [frontend API base](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/frontend/src/lib/api.ts:4).
- Schema needs tighter constraints/indexes. `kind TEXT` has no check constraint, `PRIMARY KEY (vm_id, ts)` ignores the discriminator, and `kind, ts` is not the endpoint access path. Use `CHECK (kind IN (...))`, persist `account_id`/`host_id`, and index `(account_id, kind, vm_id, ts)`.
- Ingest validation is missing. “Up to ~200 points” is only prose. Backend must enforce max batch size/body size, timestamp skew bounds, nonnegative memory, sane CPU bounds, duplicate semantics, and no future partitions created by malicious timestamps. See [wire format](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:95).
- Sampler needs reset behavior. If a cgroup disappears and later reappears with the same unit name, `cpu.stat` can reset while `prev` still contains old usage, producing negative CPU. Add pruning/reset tests around VM stop/start. See [discovery](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:67) and [prev map](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:82).
- Browser VM UI target is wrong. `BrowserVMLivePage` is a full-screen iframe proxy route, not a detail page with tabs. There is no `/browser-vms/:id` detail route today. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:204) and existing routes in [App.tsx](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/frontend/src/App.tsx:78).

**MINOR**
- `CurrentFeature.md` still says the new tab is “Usage”, while the design correctly says “Resources”. Align it before implementation to avoid agents touching the existing billing `UsageTab`. See [design](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/plans/2026-04-29-vm-instrumentation-design.md:17) and [CurrentFeature.md](/home/mantiz/OpenClawMachines/docs/CurrentFeature.md:11).
- Test plan should explicitly add: concurrent partition ensure/drop with advisory lock, UTC midnight partition rollover, no-partition insert failure behavior, late downsample reruns, SSE reconnect after Cloud Run EOF, duplicate backfill de-dupe by timestamp, and context cancellation freeing SSE handlers.
- If Recharts is added, call out the lockfile update and bundle impact. It is not currently in `frontend/package.json`.
